📰 本期新闻动态 / News Brief

  • 本期狂犬病病毒新闻集中呈现以下动态。
  • 《Communications Health》发表的一项研究表明,官方记录大大低估了美国人类 狂犬病 病例的数量。从 2000 年到 2024 年,CDC 平均每年仅诊断出 2.4 例病例,但研究人员估计,超过 90% 的病例未被发现,预计确诊病例约为 856 例,而确诊病例为 60 例。
  • 据农业部称,南非记录了 15 人死亡 狂犬病。其中包括东开普省的 7 例确诊病例和 2 例疑似病例、夸赞省的 5 例确诊病例和林波波省的 1 例确诊病例。该事件是根据农业部的报告得到证实的。 [news-9ffaf2bd6944649e]。
  • 沙利文县公共卫生部门证实,利文斯顿庄园的一只蝙蝠检测出 狂犬病病毒 呈阳性。这是今年首例涉及经实验室验证呈阳性动物的人类接触事件。 [news-d7247590aa1638b9]。
  • 世卫组织确认不丹消除了由狗传播的人类 狂犬病。 [news-a029ae619af9eec3]。
  • This reporting window highlights the following Rabies virus news developments.
  • A study published in Communications Health suggests that official records significantly underestimate the number of human rabies cases in the US. [news-1c426109f66cd034].
  • South Africa recorded 15 human rabies deaths, according to the Department of Agriculture.
  • A bat in Livingston Manor tested positive for the rabies virus, as confirmed by the Sullivan County Public Health Department.
  • WHO validates Bhutan for eliminating dog-transmitted human rabies, confirmed. [news-a029ae619af9eec3].

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Journal of Immunological Methods  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-09-01  |  DOI: 10.1016/j.jim.2026.114113  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 114113

用于检测人脑脊液中 狂犬病病毒 糖蛋白抗体的间接 ELISA 的开发和诊断评估

Development and diagnostic evaluation of indirect ELISA for detecting antibodies against the rabies virus glycoprotein in human cerebrospinal fluid
作者： Helena Ya Luen Wong, Elaine Raniero Fernandes, Fernanda Guedes, Sandriana dos Ramos Silva
摘要中文翻译准确检测针对 狂犬病病毒 (RABV) 的鞘内抗体对于死前 诊断 和疾病监测至关重要,特别是当病毒中和活​​性较低或延迟时。在本研究中,开发了间接 ELISA 测定法来检测脑脊液中针对 RABV 糖蛋白 (RABV G) 的 IgM、IgG 和多价 (IgA/IgM/IgG) 抗体。通过 SDS-PAGE、单克隆抗体识别和凝集素结合测定证实,通过Lens culinaris 凝集素亲和层析纯化的 RABV G 抗原保留了其理化和免疫反应特性。分析验证表明检测限低 (0.056-0.063)、高精度和重现性高(测定内 CV ≤ 1.84%)、可接受的回收率(101.1-115.6%)、线性响应(R 2 高达 0.989)和最小的基质效应。使用复合参考标准的诊断性能显示 IgM (AUC 0.8150) 和 IgG (AUC 0.7917) 具有中等一致性,而多价形式 (AUC 0.9695) 具有出色的性能,具有最高的灵敏度 (88.6%) 和总体一致性 (84.8%)。对 ELISA 阳性样品的 RFFIT 分析表明,部分 IgM 阳性病例以及较高比例的 IgG 和多价阳性样品具有中和活性。所有 RFFIT 阳性样本在多价 ELISA 格式中均呈阳性。一名患者的连续脑脊液取样表明,ELISA 检测到的结合抗体先于可测量的中和活性。总体而言,基于天然 RABV G 的间接 ELISA 提供了鞘内抗体反应的灵敏检测,但它不能一致地预测病毒中和活​​性。这些发现支持结合和功能测定与分子诊断的互补使用,以对中枢神经系统中的 狂犬病 感染进行全面的生前评估。未来需要对更大的 队列研究 进行研究来定义临床适用性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
准确检测针对 狂犬病病毒 (RABV) 的鞘内抗体对于死前 诊断 和疾病监测至关重要,特别是当病毒中和活​​性较低或延迟时。
设计与对象
在本研究中,开发了间接 ELISA 测定法来检测脑脊液中针对 RABV 糖蛋白 (RABV G) 的 IgM、IgG 和多价 (IgA/IgM/IgG) 抗体。
核心方法
通过 SDS-PAGE、单克隆抗体识别和凝集素结合测定证实,通过Lens culinaris 凝集素亲和层析纯化的 RABV G 抗原保留了其理化和免疫反应特性。
主要结果
使用复合参考标准的诊断性能显示 IgM (AUC 0.8150) 和 IgG (AUC 0.7917) 具有中等一致性,而多价形式 (AUC 0.9695) 具有出色的性能,具有最高的灵敏度 (88.6%) 和总体一致性 (84.8%)。
解释与创新
对 ELISA 阳性样品的 RFFIT 分析表明,部分 IgM 阳性病例以及较高比例的 IgG 和多价阳性样品具有中和活性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持结合和功能测定与分子诊断的互补使用,以对中枢神经系统中的 狂犬病 感染进行全面的生前评估。
局限与证据强度
一名患者的连续脑脊液取样表明,ELISA 检测到的结合抗体先于可测量的中和活性。总体而言,基于天然 RABV G 的间接 ELISA 提供了鞘内抗体反应的灵敏检测,但它不能一致地预测病毒中和活​​性。

Development and diagnostic evaluation of indirect ELISA for detecting antibodies against the rabies virus glycoprotein in human cerebrospinal fluid

Authors: Helena Ya Luen Wong, Elaine Raniero Fernandes, Fernanda Guedes, Sandriana dos Ramos Silva
Original abstract (English)
The accurate detection of intrathecal antibodies against the rabies virus (RABV) is critical for antemortem diagnosis and disease monitoring, particularly when the virus-neutralizing activity is low or delayed. In this study, indirect ELISA assays were developed to detect IgM, IgG, and polyvalent (IgA/IgM/IgG) antibodies against the RABV glycoprotein (RABV G) in cerebrospinal fluid. The RABV G antigen, purified by Lens culinaris lectin affinity chromatography, retained its physicochemical and immunoreactive properties, as confirmed by SDS-PAGE, monoclonal antibody recognition and lectin-binding assays. The analytical validation demonstrated low limits of detection (0.056-0.063), high precision and reproducibility (intra-assay CV ≤ 1.84%), acceptable recovery (101.1-115.6%), linear response (R 2 up to 0.989), and minimal matrix effects. Diagnostic performance using a composite reference standard showed moderate agreement for IgM (AUC 0.8150) and IgG (AUC 0.7917), and excellent performance for the polyvalent format (AUC 0.9695), which presented the highest sensitivity (88.6%) and overall agreement (84.8%). RFFIT analysis of ELISA-positive samples demonstrated neutralizing activity in a subset of IgM-positive cases, and a higher proportion of IgG- and polyvalent-positive samples. All RFFIT-positive samples were positive in the polyvalent ELISA format. Serial CSF sampling from one patient indicated that ELISA-detected binding antibodies preceded measurable neutralizing activity. Overall, indirect ELISA based on the native RABV G provides sensitive detection of intrathecal antibody responses, but it does not consistently predict virus-neutralizing activity. These findings support the complementary use of binding and functional assays with molecular diagnostics for a comprehensive antemortem evaluation of rabies infection in the central nervous system. Future studies in larger cohorts are necessary to define the clinical applicability.

The accurate detection of intrathecal antibodies against the rabies virus (RABV) is critical for antemortem diagnosis and disease monitoring, particularly when the virus-neutralizing activity is low or delayed.
Design and population
In this study, indirect ELISA assays were developed to detect IgM, IgG, and polyvalent (IgA/IgM/IgG) antibodies against the RABV glycoprotein (RABV G) in cerebrospinal fluid.

Main results
Diagnostic performance using a composite reference standard showed moderate agreement for IgM (AUC 0.8150) and IgG (AUC 0.7917), and excellent performance for the polyvalent format (AUC 0.9695), which presented the highest sensitivity (88.6%) and overall agreement (84.8%).
Interpretation and novelty
RFFIT analysis of ELISA-positive samples demonstrated neutralizing activity in a subset of IgM-positive cases, and a higher proportion of IgG- and polyvalent-positive samples.

用于检测 狂犬病病毒 抗原的免疫组织化学染色

An Immunohistochemistry Staining for the Detection of Rabies Virus Antigens
作者： Authors unavailable
摘要中文翻译来源:Niezgoda,M. 等人,福尔马林固定组织中狂犬病病毒抗原检测的免疫组织化学测试。J. 维斯。过期。(2021) 该视频演示了感染小鼠脑组织中 狂犬病病毒 抗原检测的免疫组织化学染色。该过程包括脱蜡和酒精 治疗,然后进行蛋白水解 治疗 以增强抗原可及性。免疫组织化学染色显示红色沉淀,证实组织切片中存在 狂犬病 抗原。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在开发一种免疫组织化学方法来检测感染组织中的 狂犬病病毒 抗原,满足 狂犬病 疾病对可靠、高效的诊断工具的需求。
设计与对象
该研究描述了 2021 年进行的基于视频的免疫组织化学染色演示,用于检测受感染小鼠脑组织中的 狂犬病病毒 抗原。
核心方法
该过程包括对组织切片进行脱石蜡和酒精 治疗,然后进行蛋白水解 治疗 以增强抗原可及性,以及免疫组织化学染色以可视化 狂犬病病毒 抗原。
主要结果
免疫组织化学染色显示红色沉淀,证实组织切片中存在 狂犬病 抗原。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为免疫组织化学在检测 狂犬病病毒 抗原方面的有效性的证明,强调了其作为诊断方法的实用性。
科研与公卫意义
该研究结果为狂犬病病毒抗原检测提供了可靠的方法,可以提高诊断准确性并有助于监测和狂犬病疾病的管理。
局限与证据强度
该研究是视频演示,不包括统计分析或与其他方法的比较,这将证据的强度限制为中等。

An Immunohistochemistry Staining for the Detection of Rabies Virus Antigens

Authors: Authors unavailable
Original abstract (English)
Source: Niezgoda, M. et al., Immunohistochemistry Test for the Lyssavirus Antigen Detection from Formalin-Fixed Tissues. J. Vis. Exp. (2021) This video demonstrates immunohistochemistry staining for rabies virus antigen detection in infected mouse brain tissue. The process includes deparaffinization and alcohol treatment, followed by proteolytic treatment to enhance antigen accessibility. Immunohistochemistry staining reveals red precipitates, confirming the presence of rabies antigens in the tissue section.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to develop an immunohistochemistry method for detecting rabies virus antigens in infected tissues, addressing the need for a reliable and efficient diagnostic tool for rabies disease.
Design and population
The study describes a video-based demonstration of immunohistochemistry staining for rabies virus antigen detection in infected mouse brain tissue, conducted in 2021.
Core methods
The process involves deparaffinization and alcohol treatment of tissue sections, followed by proteolytic treatment to enhance antigen accessibility, and immunohistochemistry staining to visualize rabies virus antigens.
Main results
Immunohistochemistry staining revealed red precipitates, confirming the presence of rabies antigens in the tissue section.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as a demonstration of the effectiveness of immunohistochemistry in detecting rabies virus antigens, highlighting its utility as a diagnostic method.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide a reliable method for rabies virus antigen detection, which can improve diagnostic accuracy and aid in the surveillance and management of rabies disease.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is a video demonstration and does not include statistical analysis or comparison with other methods, which limits the strength of the evidence to moderate.

小鼠脑组织中 狂犬病病毒 感染的三维可视化

Three-Dimensional Visualization of a Rabies Virus Infection in Mouse Brain Tissue
作者： TODO TODO
摘要中文翻译资料来源:Zaeck,L. 等人。溶剂清除脑组织中 狂犬病病毒 感染的高分辨率 3D 成像。J. 维斯。过期。(2019)。该视频演示了感染 狂犬病病毒 (RABV) 的小鼠脑组织的制备和成像过程,以评估感染进展。它概述了关键步骤,包括用荧光标记进行组织染色、脱水、透明化和安装共焦激光扫描显微镜,最终生成三维表示。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在利用先进的成像技术可视化小鼠脑组织中 狂犬病病毒 感染的进展,以更好地了解该疾病的 发病机制。
设计与对象
该研究涉及对感染 狂犬病病毒 (RABV) 的小鼠脑组织进行制备和成像,以评估感染的空间和时间进展。该过程包括组织染色、脱水、透明化和共焦激光扫描显微镜安装。
核心方法
所描述的方法包括使用荧光标记进行组织染色,然后进行脱水、透明化和封固程序以准备用于成像的脑组织。采用共焦激光扫描显微镜生成感染的三维表示。
主要结果
研究中概述的过程成功地生成了小鼠脑组织中 狂犬病病毒 感染的三维表示,通过概述的步骤展示了感染的进展。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为高分辨率 3D 成像在可视化 狂犬病病毒 感染进展方面的有效性的证明,强调了该方法在提供对感染空间动态的详细见解方面的新颖性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为开发更准确的诊断工具提供信息,并增强对 狂犬病病毒 发病机制 的理解,这对于改进动物和人类 狂犬病 病例中的 监测 和 治疗 策略至关重要。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于展示技术过程,而不是提供临床或流行病学数据,并且证据基于单一方法学方法。由于缺乏比较或更广泛的数据,研究结果的可信度较低。

Three-Dimensional Visualization of a Rabies Virus Infection in Mouse Brain Tissue

Authors: TODO TODO
Original abstract (English)
Source: Zaeck, L., et al. High-Resolution 3D Imaging of Rabies Virus Infection in Solvent-Cleared Brain Tissue. J. Vis. Exp. (2019). This video demonstrates the process of preparing and imaging mouse brain tissue infected with the rabies virus (RABV) to evaluate infection progression. It outlines key steps, including tissue staining with fluorescent markers, dehydration, clearing, and mounting for confocal laser scanning microscopy, culminating in the generation of a three-dimensional representation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to visualize the progression of rabies virus infection in mouse brain tissue using advanced imaging techniques to better understand the pathogenesis of the disease.
Design and population
The study involved the preparation and imaging of mouse brain tissue infected with the rabies virus (RABV) to evaluate the spatial and temporal progression of infection. The process included tissue staining, dehydration, clearing, and mounting for confocal laser scanning microscopy.
Core methods
The methods described include the use of fluorescent markers for tissue staining, followed by dehydration, clearing, and mounting procedures to prepare the brain tissue for imaging. Confocal laser scanning microscopy was employed to generate a three-dimensional representation of the infection.
Main results
The process outlined in the study successfully generated a three-dimensional representation of rabies virus infection in mouse brain tissue, demonstrating the progression of the infection through the outlined steps.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as a demonstration of the effectiveness of high-resolution 3D imaging in visualizing rabies virus infection progression, highlighting the novelty of this method in providing detailed insights into the spatial dynamics of the infection.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of more accurate diagnostic tools and enhance understanding of rabies virus pathogenesis, which is critical for improving surveillance and treatment strategies in both animal and human rabies cases.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to demonstrating a technical process rather than providing clinical or epidemiological data, and the evidence is based on a single methodological approach. The confidence in the findings is moderate due to the lack of comparative or broader data.

Journal: Journal of the American Veterinary Medical Association  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-31  |  DOI: 10.2460/javma.26.07.0545  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 1-8

2024 年期间在美国的 狂犬病 监测

Rabies surveillance in the United States during 2024
作者： Ravikiran Keshavamurthy, Cassandra Boutelle, Sarah C. Bonaparte, Crystal M. Gigante, Rene Edgar Condori, Rebecca Earnest, Jordona D. Kirby, Kathleen M. Nelson, Lillian A. Orciari, Yasmeen B. Ross
摘要中文翻译摘要 目的 描述 2024 年美国 狂犬病 和 狂犬病 检测的流行病学情况。方法 美国国家 狂犬病 狂犬病 系统 (NRSS) 每月收集约 130 个公共卫生、农业和学术实验室的动物 狂犬病 检测结果。对 2024 年报告的数据进行了分析,以检查 狂犬病 变体 (RVV) 动物 狂犬病 病例的时间和地理趋势。结果 2024 年,NRSS 共报告动物 狂犬病 病例 3,834 例(占提交的 90,343 份样本的 4.2%),比 2023 年增加 2.0%。阳性样本中,家畜 331 例(8.6%),野生动物 3,503 例(91.4%)。最常见的患有狂犬病的家畜是猫(243 例阳性[1.3% 阳性率]),最常见的野生动物是蝙蝠(1,376 例阳性[5.7% 阳性率]),其次是浣熊(1,080 例[10.1% 阳性率])。2024 年,两辆非地方性 RVV 进入美国。四人死于 狂犬病,其中 2 人曾接触过蝙蝠但未得到治疗,还有 1 人的病例涉及国际旅行。结论 地方性 RVV 历史范围的扩大可能导致巨大的公共卫生和动物健康负担。2024 年国内获得的人类 狂犬病 病例强调需要持续进行有关 狂犬病 暴露风险的公众教育,以确保及时有效地实施暴露后预防。国际上获得的人类 狂犬病 病例表明,美国仍然容易受到人类和动物 狂犬病 进口的影响。临床相关性 NRSS 通过监测美国的 狂犬病 趋势,在保护公众和动物健康方面发挥着至关重要的作用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在描述 2024 年美国 狂犬病 和 狂犬病 检测的流行病学状况,解决了解 狂犬病 趋势及其影响的科学和公共卫生问题。
设计与对象
该研究分析了来自美国国家 狂犬病 狂犬病 系统 (NRSS) 的数据,该系统每月从大约 130 个公共卫生、农业和学术实验室收集动物 狂犬病 检测结果。2024 年,报告了 3,834 例动物 狂犬病 病例,占提交的 90,343 份样本的 4.2%。
核心方法
对 2024 年报告的数据进行了分析,以检查 狂犬病 变体 (RVV) 动物 狂犬病 病例的时间和地理趋势。
主要结果
2024年,向NRSS报告了3,834例动物狂犬病病例(占提交的90,343份样本的4.2%),比2023年增加了2.0%。在阳性样本中,331例(8.6%)是家畜,3,503例(91.4%)是野生动物。最常见的患有狂犬病的家畜是猫(243 例阳性[1.3% 阳性率]),最常见的野生动物是蝙蝠(1,376 例阳性[5.7% 阳性率]),其次是浣熊(1,080 例[10.1% 阳性率])。
解释与创新
地方性RVV历史范围的扩大可能会导致巨大的公共卫生和动物健康负担。研究结果强调了监测 狂犬病 趋势以应对新出现风险的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
2024 年国内获得的人类 狂犬病 病例强调需要持续进行有关 狂犬病 暴露风险的公众教育,以确保及时有效地实施暴露后预防。国际上获得的人类 狂犬病 病例表明,美国仍然容易受到人类和动物 狂犬病 进口的影响。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告研究设计或数据分析的具体限制。证据强度中等,因为研究结果基于 监测 数据,不包括实验或因果证据。

Rabies surveillance in the United States during 2024

Authors: Ravikiran Keshavamurthy, Cassandra Boutelle, Sarah C. Bonaparte, Crystal M. Gigante, Rene Edgar Condori, Rebecca Earnest, Jordona D. Kirby, Kathleen M. Nelson, Lillian A. Orciari, Yasmeen B. Ross
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Objective Describe the epidemiologic landscape of rabies and rabies testing in the US during 2024. Methods Animal rabies testing results from approximately 130 public health, agriculture, and academic laboratories are collected by the US National Rabies Surveillance System (NRSS) each month. Data reported in 2024 were analyzed to examine temporal and geographic trends in animal rabies cases by rabies virus variant (RVV). Results In 2024, 3,834 cases of animal rabies were reported to the NRSS (4.2% of 90,343 samples submitted), representing a 2.0% increase from 2023. Of positive samples, 331 (8.6%) were domestic animals, and 3,503 (91.4%) were wildlife. The most commonly found rabid domestic animal was cats (243 positive [1.3% positivity]), and the most commonly found wildlife were bats (1,376 positive [5.7% positivity]) followed by raccoons (1,080 [10.1% positivity]). Two nonenzootic RVVs gained entry into the US in 2024. Four people died from rabies, including 2 who experienced bat exposures that were left untreated and 1 person whose case involved international travel. Conclusions The expansion of the historical range of enzootic RVVs can lead to substantial public health and animal health burden. Domestically acquired human rabies cases in 2024 underscore the need for continual public education on the risks of rabies exposures to ensure timely and effective administration of postexposure prophylaxis. The internationally acquired human rabies case demonstrates that the US remains vulnerable to human and animal rabies importations. Clinical Relevance The NRSS plays a crucial role in protecting public and animal health by monitoring rabies trends in the US.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to describe the epidemiologic landscape of rabies and rabies testing in the US during 2024, addressing the scientific and public health problem of understanding rabies trends and their implications.
Design and population
The study analyzed data from the US National Rabies Surveillance System (NRSS), which collects animal rabies testing results from approximately 130 public health, agriculture, and academic laboratories each month. In 2024, 3,834 cases of animal rabies were reported, representing 4.2% of 90,343 samples submitted.
Core methods
Data reported in 2024 were analyzed to examine temporal and geographic trends in animal rabies cases by rabies virus variant (RVV).
Main results
In 2024, 3,834 cases of animal rabies were reported to the NRSS (4.2% of 90,343 samples submitted), representing a 2.0% increase from 2023. Of positive samples, 331 (8.6%) were domestic animals, and 3,503 (91.4%) were wildlife. The most commonly found rabid domestic animal was cats (243 positive [1.3% positivity]), and the most commonly found wildlife were bats (1,376 positive [5.7% positivity]) followed by raccoons (1,080 [10.1% positivity]).
Interpretation and novelty
The expansion of the historical range of enzootic RVVs can lead to substantial public health and animal health burden. The findings highlight the importance of monitoring rabies trends to address emerging risks.
Scientific and public-health significance
Domestically acquired human rabies cases in 2024 underscore the need for continual public education on the risks of rabies exposures to ensure timely and effective administration of postexposure prophylaxis. The internationally acquired human rabies case demonstrates that the US remains vulnerable to human and animal rabies importations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations to the study design or data analysis. The evidence strength is moderate, as the findings are based on surveillance data and do not include experimental or causal evidence.

多种细胞因子信号通路的联合破坏使水疱性口炎病毒的一步顺行追踪成为可能。

Combined Disruption of Multiple Cytokine Signaling Pathways Enables One-Step Anterograde Tracing with Vesicular Stomatitis Virus.
作者： Xiang Ma, Constance L. Cepko
摘要中文翻译摘要 定义选定神经元群的直接突触后目标仍然是神经回路映射的主要挑战。水疱性口炎病毒 (VSV) 可沿顺行方向有效传播,但具有复制能力的 VSV 会经历多步传播,因此无法区分直接下游目标和间接目标。在这里,我们开发了一种基于糖蛋白删除的 VSV (VSVdG) 的策略,使用 AAV 介导的反式互补和多种适应进行一步顺行追踪。在该系统中,VSVdG被设计为编码Cre,使得Cre依赖性AAV仅在VSVdG感染相同细胞后才表达VSV-G,从而将VSV-G的表达限制在较短的时间窗口内。为了减少 VSV-M 介导的细胞毒性,我们引入了 M33A/M51R 双突变 VSV-Md 变体。使用基底神经节回路作为模型,我们发现有效的一步式 VSVdG 传播需要破坏由 AAV 转导和 VSV 感染触发的细胞因子介导的 抗病毒药物 反应。这是通过 IFNAR1 敲除小鼠中 I 型干扰素信号传导的丧失,以及通过 AAV 介导的 CVS-N2c 狂犬病病毒 磷蛋白表达或施用细胞因子阻断抗体混合物来抑制额外的 抗病毒药物 反应来实现的。这些适应使 VSVdG 从两性小鼠的背侧纹状体传播到预期的下游目标。然而,下游标记细胞包括神经元和神经胶质细胞,揭示了将这种方法解释为严格的神经元到神经元单突触顺行传播的一个重要限制。总体而言,本研究不是提供一种即用型追踪工具,而是提出了一种用于一步顺行回路追踪的 VSVdG 策略,并定义了病毒毒性、先天免疫和细胞类型特异性限制,开发一种高效且特异性的单突触顺行病毒示踪剂必须解决这些限制。意义陈述 绘制确定的神经元群体的直接下游目标对于理解神经回路功能至关重要,但可靠的单突触顺行病毒示踪剂仍然有限。我们开发了一种基于 VSVdG 的策略,该策略使用 AAV 介导的反式互补在短时间内限制起始细胞中 VSV-G 的表达,并合并 VSV-M 变体以降低毒性。在小鼠基底神经节中,当先天 抗病毒药物 屏障受到抑制时,该系统能够实现从背侧纹状体到预期输出区域的一步传播。我们的结果确定 I 型干扰素和其他 I/II 型干扰素独立的细胞因子信号传导是 VSVdG 传播的主要限制。这项研究为基于 VSV 的一步顺行追踪建立了原理证明,同时定义了需要进一步改进的病毒毒性、先天免疫和细胞类型特异性限制。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决定义选定神经元群体的直接突触后目标以进行神经回路映射的挑战。
设计与对象
该研究涉及开发一种基于 VSVdG 的策略,在两性小鼠中使用 AAV 介导的反式互补进行一步顺行追踪。
核心方法
该方法包括改造VSVdG以编码Cre,允许Cre依赖性AAV仅在VSVdG感染相同细胞后才表达VSV-G,并引入VSV-M变体以降低细胞毒性。
主要结果
当先天 抗病毒药物 屏障被抑制时,在小鼠中观察到从背侧纹状体到预期下游目标的有效一步 VSVdG 传播。
解释与创新
作者将他们的发现解释为为基于 VSV 的一步顺行追踪建立了原理证明,同时确定了与病毒毒性和先天免疫相关的限制。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为开发用于神经回路研究的更有效和特异性的单突触顺行病毒示踪剂提供了信息。
局限与证据强度
研究表明,下游标记细胞包括神经元和神经胶质细胞,这表明将该方法解释为严格的神经元到神经元单突触顺行传播存在局限性。

Combined Disruption of Multiple Cytokine Signaling Pathways Enables One-Step Anterograde Tracing with Vesicular Stomatitis Virus.

Authors: Xiang Ma, Constance L. Cepko
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Defining the direct postsynaptic targets of selected neuronal populations remains a major challenge for neural circuit mapping. Vesicular stomatitis virus (VSV) spreads efficiently in the anterograde direction, but replication-competent VSV undergoes multistep spread and therefore cannot distinguish direct from indirect downstream targets. Here, we developed a glycoprotein-deleted VSV(VSVdG)-based strategy for one-step anterograde tracing using AAV-mediated trans-complementation with several adaptations. In this system, VSVdG was engineered to encode Cre, allowing a Cre-dependent AAV to express VSV-G only after VSVdG infected the same cells, thereby limiting VSV-G expression to a short time window. To reduce VSV-M-mediated cytotoxicity, we introduced the M33A/M51R double-mutant VSV-Md variant. Using the basal ganglia circuit as a model, we found that efficient one-step VSVdG spread required disruption of cytokine-mediated antiviral responses triggered by AAV transduction and VSV infection. This was achieved by loss of type I interferon signaling in IFNAR1-knockout mice, together with suppression of additional antiviral responses either through AAV-mediated expression of the CVS-N2c rabies virus phosphoprotein or administration of cytokine-blocking antibody cocktail. These adaptations enabled VSVdG spread from dorsal striatum to expected downstream targets in mice of both sexes. However, the downstream labeled cells included both neurons and glia, revealing an important limitation for interpreting this approach as strictly neuron-to-neuron monosynaptic anterograde spread. Overall, rather than providing a ready-to-use tracing tool, this study presents a VSVdG strategy for one-step anterograde circuit tracing, and defines viral toxicity, innate immunity, and cell-type specificity constraints that must be addressed to develop an efficient and specific monosynaptic anterograde viral tracer. Significance Statement Mapping direct downstream targets of defined neuronal populations is essential for understanding neural circuit function, but reliable monosynaptic anterograde viral tracers remain limited. We developed a VSVdG-based strategy that uses AAV-mediated trans-complementation to restrict VSV-G expression to starter cells in a short time window and incorporates VSV-M variant to reduce toxicity. In the mouse basal ganglia, this system enabled one-step spread from dorsal striatum to expected output regions when innate antiviral barriers were suppressed. Our results identify type I interferon and additional type I/type II interferon-independent cytokine signaling as major restrictions of VSVdG spread. This study establishes proof of principle for VSV-based one-step anterograde tracing while defining viral toxicity, innate immunity, and cell-type specificity constraints in need of further improvement.
View research seven-element analysis

The study aimed to address the challenge of defining direct postsynaptic targets of selected neuronal populations for neural circuit mapping.
Design and population
The research involved developing a VSVdG-based strategy for one-step anterograde tracing using AAV-mediated trans-complementation in mice of both sexes.
Core methods
The method included engineering VSVdG to encode Cre, allowing Cre-dependent AAV to express VSV-G only after VSVdG infected the same cells, and introducing a VSV-M variant to reduce cytotoxicity.
Main results
Efficient one-step VSVdG spread was observed from dorsal striatum to expected downstream targets in mice when innate antiviral barriers were suppressed.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted their findings as establishing a proof of principle for VSV-based one-step anterograde tracing while identifying constraints related to viral toxicity and innate immunity.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the development of more efficient and specific monosynaptic anterograde viral tracers for neural circuit studies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study revealed that downstream labeled cells included both neurons and glia, indicating a limitation in interpreting the approach as strictly neuron-to-neuron monosynaptic anterograde spread.

基于FISH可解码CASS条形码的空间分辨突触连接组映射

Spatially resolved synaptic connectome mapping with FISH-decodable CASS barcodes.
作者： Yi-Jun Zhu, Ya-Qian Wang, Hua-Tai Xu
摘要中文翻译绘制神经元连接图对于理解神经回路的结构和功能至关重要。虽然使用条形码 狂犬病病毒 的高通量、经济高效的方法可以提供有价值的细胞水平见解,但它们受到条形码测序空间分辨率的限制。为了提供一种与荧光原位杂交 (FISH) 兼容的空间条形码读出替代方案,我们开发了 CASS(人工短序列组合)条形码,这是一种抗错误且可原位杂交检测的工具。通过将 CASS 条形码与 狂犬病病毒 单突触追踪相结合,我们可以通过基于 FISH 的条形码解码实现原位连接组映射。使用这种方法,我们在三只小鼠中以空间精度识别出了 1,532 个与初级视觉皮层神经元连接的突触对,证明了其效率和可扩展性。该方法提供了一种基于 FISH 的方法,用于识别神经元与空间背景的连接性,并可能促进连接性映射与分子分析的未来整合,从而增进我们对神经回路的理解。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
绘制神经元连接图对于理解神经回路的结构和功能至关重要。该研究旨在通过开发一种空间分辨连接组图谱的新方法来解决当前方法的局限性。
设计与对象
该研究涉及三只小鼠,重点关注初级视觉皮层。它将 CASS 条形码与 狂犬病病毒 单突触追踪相结合,通过基于 FISH 的条形码解码实现原位连接组映射。
核心方法
这些方法包括开发 CASS 条形码作为空间条形码读出的 FISH 兼容替代方案。这些条形码与 狂犬病病毒 单突触追踪相结合,以高精度绘制突触连接图。
主要结果
使用开发的方法,该研究确定了三只小鼠的 1,532 个与初级视觉皮层神经元连接的突触对,证明了该方法的效率和可扩展性。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为该方法在为神经元连接提供空间背景方面的有效性的证据。这种方法代表了一种将连接图谱与分子分析相结合的新方法。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能为未来对神经回路及其分子基础的研究提供信息,从而有可能增进我们对大脑功能和疾病机制的理解。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于三只小鼠和特定的大脑区域,这可能会影响普遍性。证据是中等的,因为结果是基于单一的实验方法而没有更广泛的验证。

Spatially resolved synaptic connectome mapping with FISH-decodable CASS barcodes.

Authors: Yi-Jun Zhu, Ya-Qian Wang, Hua-Tai Xu
Original abstract (English)
Mapping neuronal connectivity is essential for understanding the structure and function of neural circuits. While high-throughput, cost-effective methods using barcoded rabies viruses provide valuable cellular-level insights, they are limited by the spatial resolution of barcode sequencing. To provide a fluorescent in situ hybridization (FISH)-compatible alternative for spatial barcode readout, we developed the CASS (combination of artificial short sequences) barcode, an error-robust and in situ-hybridization-detectable tool. By combining CASS barcoding with rabies virus monosynaptic tracing, we enable in situ connectome mapping through FISH-based barcode decoding. Using this approach, we identified 1,532 synaptic pairs connected to neurons in the primary visual cortex with spatial precision across three mice, demonstrating its efficiency and scalability. This method provides a FISH-based approach for identifying neuronal connectivity with spatial context and may facilitate future integration of connectivity mapping with molecular profiling, thereby advancing our understanding of neural circuits.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Mapping neuronal connectivity is essential for understanding the structure and function of neural circuits. The study aimed to address the limitations of current methods by developing a new approach for spatially resolved connectome mapping.
Design and population
The study involved three mice and focused on the primary visual cortex. It combined CASS barcoding with rabies virus monosynaptic tracing to enable in situ connectome mapping through FISH-based barcode decoding.
Core methods
The methods included the development of CASS barcodes as a FISH-compatible alternative for spatial barcode readout. These barcodes were combined with rabies virus monosynaptic tracing to map synaptic connectivity with high precision.
Main results
Using the developed approach, the study identified 1,532 synaptic pairs connected to neurons in the primary visual cortex across three mice, demonstrating the efficiency and scalability of the method.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as evidence of the method's effectiveness in providing spatial context for neuronal connectivity. This approach represents a novel way to integrate connectivity mapping with molecular profiling.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform future research on neural circuits and their molecular underpinnings, potentially advancing our understanding of brain function and disease mechanisms.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to three mice and a specific brain region, which may affect generalizability. The evidence is moderate, as the results are based on a single experimental approach without broader validation.

伏隔核的单神经元连接组。

Single-neuron connectome of nucleus accumbens.
作者： Yuan Liu, Wei Song, Xiaoxue Shi, Qiumiao Wang, Wanqiu Zhou, Qing Li, Biyu Ren, Xueyan Jia, Xiangning Li, Hui Gong
摘要中文翻译伏隔核 (NAc) 协调一系列关键的生理功能,包括奖励和动机,但其输入和输出的单神经元级连接仍然不那么全面。在这里,我们以单细胞分辨率研究了成年雄性小鼠 NAc 上中型多棘神经元 (MSN) 输入和投射的组织原理。我们的结果揭示了这些神经元及其末梢在无名质(SI)细分中的拓扑分布原理。狂犬病病毒 追踪发现 Tac2 +(D1 亚型)与 Calcr +(D2 亚型)MSN 跨 NAc 亚区的单突触输入不同。此外,投射到 NAc 核心或外壳的皮质神经元显示出不同的皮质起源和侧支关系。投射到 NAc 的海马神经元优先支配具有纵向地形的核心和内侧壳,并且对下丘脑的共投射最强。这些数据共同描绘了 NAc 中不同神经元亚型的神经元连接模式,为进一步了解 NAc 功能作用背后的神经回路机制提供了解剖学基础。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在表征成年雄性小鼠伏隔核(NAc)的单神经元水平连接性,重点关注其在奖赏和动机中的作用,这些是关键的生理功能。
设计与对象
该研究在成年雄性小鼠中进行,以单细胞分辨率检查中型多棘神经元 (MSN) 在 NAc 上的输入和投射的组织原理。
核心方法
狂犬病病毒 追踪用于揭示 NAc 亚区不同 MSN 亚型的单突触输入,揭示它们的连接模式。
主要结果
结果表明,NAc 中的 Tac2 +(D1 亚型)和 Calcr +(D2 亚型)MSN 具有不同的单突触输入,投射到 NAc 核心或外壳的皮质神经元表现出不同的皮质起源和侧支关系。
解释与创新
这些发现提供了 NAc 中神经元连接模式的详细图谱,为了解其功能作用背后的神经回路机制提供了新的见解。
科研与公卫意义
这些数据有助于对 NAc 神经回路的解剖学理解,这可能为未来奖励和动机机制的研究提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于成年雄性小鼠,结果可能无法推广到其他物种或条件,但由于详细的单细胞分辨率方法,证据是强有力的。

Single-neuron connectome of nucleus accumbens.

Authors: Yuan Liu, Wei Song, Xiaoxue Shi, Qiumiao Wang, Wanqiu Zhou, Qing Li, Biyu Ren, Xueyan Jia, Xiangning Li, Hui Gong
Original abstract (English)
The nucleus accumbens (NAc) orchestrates a range of critical physiological functions, including reward and motivation, yet single-neuron-level connectivity of its inputs and outputs remains less comprehensively characterized. Here, we examined the organizing principle of input and projection of medium spiny neurons (MSNs) across NAc in adult male mice at single-cell resolution. Our results revealed topographic distribution principles of these neurons and their terminals in the substantia innominata (SI) subdivision. Rabies virus tracing uncovered different monosynaptic inputs to Tac2 + (D1 subtype) versus Calcr + (D2 subtype) MSNs across NAc subregions. Furthermore, cortical neurons projecting to NAc core or shell showed distinct cortical origins and collateralization. Hippocampal neurons projecting to the NAc preferentially innervate the core and medial shell with longitudinal topography and the strongest co-projection to the hypothalamus. Together, these data delineated the neuronal connectivity patterns of different neuronal subtypes in NAc, providing an anatomical basis for further understanding the neural circuit mechanisms underlying the functional role of NAc.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to characterize the single-neuron-level connectivity of the nucleus accumbens (NAc) in adult male mice, focusing on its role in reward and motivation, which are critical physiological functions.
Design and population
The study was conducted in adult male mice, examining the organizing principle of input and projection of medium spiny neurons (MSNs) across the NAc at single-cell resolution.
Core methods
Rabies virus tracing was used to uncover monosynaptic inputs to different MSN subtypes across NAc subregions, revealing their connectivity patterns.
Main results
The results showed that Tac2 + (D1 subtype) and Calcr + (D2 subtype) MSNs in the NAc have distinct monosynaptic inputs, with cortical neurons projecting to the NAc core or shell showing different cortical origins and collateralization.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings provide a detailed map of neuronal connectivity patterns in the NAc, offering new insights into the neural circuit mechanisms that underlie its functional role.
Scientific and public-health significance
These data contribute to the anatomical understanding of the NAc's neural circuits, which may inform future studies on reward and motivation mechanisms.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to adult male mice, and the results may not be generalizable to other species or conditions, but the evidence is strong due to the detailed single-cell resolution approach.

基因组 撇读解决了普通吸血蝙蝠(Desmodus rotundus)的新热带种群结构和线粒体核不一致问题

Genome skimming resolves Neotropical population structure and mitonuclear discordance in the common vampire bat (Desmodus rotundus)
作者： Lexi E. Frank, Marissa S. Milstein, Christopher Shaffer, Philip Suse, Steven Suse, Achaicus Chekema, Evan J. Kipp, Roxanne J. Larsen, Tiffany M. Wolf, Christopher Faulk
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查普通吸血蝙蝠(Desmodus rotundus)的种群结构和线粒体核不一致,以更好地了解其进化历史和在疾病 传播(特别是 狂犬病病毒)中的潜在作用。
设计与对象
该研究涉及 基因组 对 9 个国家的 67 个 D. rotundus 个体进行撇读,以表征核和线粒体基因组多样性。
核心方法
研究人员使用基于纳米孔的全 基因组 略读,覆盖范围约为 0.35 倍,并使用 ANGSD 软件分析数据。他们还组装了 28 个完整的线粒体基因组,并生成了 COI 和 CYTB 的共有基因。
主要结果
分析揭示了整个新热带区的线粒体核不一致模式,基因组 范围内的 F ST 值范围为 0.114 至 0.358。核PCA和祖先模型显示出连续的地理梯度,而线粒体基因组系统发育表明高度结构化、孤立的区域谱系。
解释与创新
线粒体核不一致表明男性介导的基因流动,并将巴拿马确定为关键走廊,将安第斯山脉确定为基因流动的主要障碍,支持更新世避难所中的种群隔离。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现完善了 Desmodus 的进化历史,并证明了 基因组 撇读在解决野生动物谱系方面的效用,这可以为未来的 狂犬病病毒 传播 和生态学研究提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究的结果基于 基因组 略读,覆盖范围约为 0.35 倍,这可能会限制遗传变异的分辨率。由于基因组数据可能不完整,证据强度中等。

Genome skimming resolves Neotropical population structure and mitonuclear discordance in the common vampire bat (Desmodus rotundus)

Authors: Lexi E. Frank, Marissa S. Milstein, Christopher Shaffer, Philip Suse, Steven Suse, Achaicus Chekema, Evan J. Kipp, Roxanne J. Larsen, Tiffany M. Wolf, Christopher Faulk
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Across the Neotropics, the common vampire bat ( Desmodus rotundus ) is an obligate hematophage, feeding on wildlife, domestic animals, and occasionally humans. While Desmodus is ecologically significant (e.g., nutrient cycling, complex sociality, parasitism), its role as a pathogen reservoir and vector (i.e., rabies virus) poses risks to animal and human health and local economies. Traditional single-gene and mitochondrial markers suggest high intra-species diversity within Desmodus ; however, next-generation sequencing and bioinformatic advancements enable comprehensive whole-genome analyses to resolve complex evolutionary histories. Here, we employed nanopore-based whole-genome skimming (~ 0.35X coverage) of 67 D. rotundus individuals across nine countries to characterize both nuclear and mitochondrial genomic diversity. Approximately 43 million mapped reads and 41 million loci informed our genotype likelihood analysis with the ANGSD: Analysis of Next Generation Sequencing Data software. We additionally assembled 28 complete mitogenomes along with 61 COI and 60 CYTB consensus genes. Our analysis revealed mitonuclear discordance patterns across the Neotropics. Genome-wide F ST values ranged from 0.114 to 0.358, with nuclear PCA and ancestry population structure modeling clustering populations along a continuous geographic gradient and mapping a latitudinal genetic transition zone through Panama. Conversely, mitogenomic phylogenies and maternal population structure models resolved highly structured, isolated regional lineages, including maternal divergence across the Ecuadorian Andes and localized matrilineal sorting in Guyana. This mitonuclear discordance suggests male-mediated gene flow and identifies Panama as a critical corridor and the Andes as a primary barrier to gene flow, supporting population isolation in Pleistocene refugia. Our findings refine the evolutionary history of Desmodus and demonstrate the utility of genome skimming for resolving wildlife lineages.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the population structure and mitonuclear discordance in the common vampire bat (Desmodus rotundus) to better understand its evolutionary history and potential role in disease transmission, particularly rabies virus.
Design and population
The study involved genome skimming of 67 D. rotundus individuals across nine countries to characterize both nuclear and mitochondrial genomic diversity.
Core methods
The researchers used nanopore-based whole-genome skimming with ~0.35X coverage and analyzed the data using ANGSD software. They also assembled 28 complete mitogenomes and generated consensus genes for COI and CYTB.
Main results
The analysis revealed mitonuclear discordance patterns across the Neotropics, with genome-wide F ST values ranging from 0.114 to 0.358. Nuclear PCA and ancestry modeling showed a continuous geographic gradient, while mitogenomic phylogenies indicated highly structured, isolated regional lineages.
Interpretation and novelty
The mitonuclear discordance suggests male-mediated gene flow and identifies Panama as a critical corridor and the Andes as a primary barrier to gene flow, supporting population isolation in Pleistocene refugia.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings refine the evolutionary history of Desmodus and demonstrate the utility of genome skimming for resolving wildlife lineages, which can inform future studies on rabies virus transmission and ecology.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's results are based on genome skimming with ~0.35X coverage, which may limit the resolution of genetic variation. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the potential for incomplete genomic data.

Peer Review Report For: Development and characterization of pseudotype-based microneutralization assay to assess functionality of anti-rabies protein G antibodies [version 1; peer review: 2 approved with reservations]
作者： Antonelli R#, Vezzani G#, Guerrini N, Giovagnoli V, Dapporto F, Iturriza M, Loomis R, Montomoli E, Rossi O, Molesti E
摘要中文翻译背景 狂犬病病毒 (RABV) 是一种嗜神经性人畜共患病原体,每年估计导致 60,000 人死亡。尽管 疫苗接种 计划广泛传播,但 狂犬病 仍然是巨大的公共卫生负担,特别是在 暴露后预防 (PEP) 获取机会有限的地区。用于评估 狂犬病 疫苗 免疫原性的标准血清学测定,包括快速荧光聚焦抑制试验 (RFFIT) 和荧光抗体病毒中和 (FAVN) 试验,需要在生物安全 3 级 (BSL-3) 条件下处理活病毒,限制了其可扩展性和更广泛的实施。方法我们开发并表征了一种基于假型的微中和测定,使用慢病毒 HIV-1 主链生成表达来自三种抗原不同的 狂犬病病毒 菌株(CTN-1 V5、CVS-11 和巴斯德)的糖蛋白的假型病毒颗粒。中和测定在 BSL-2 条件下进行,并从灵敏度、特异性和高通量应用的适用性方面评估测定性能。结果基于假型的测定能够灵敏且可重复地检测三种糖蛋白变体的 狂犬病病毒 中和抗体。该平台在 BSL-2 遏制下表现出强大的性能,支持高效且可扩展的测试。包含抗原性不同的菌株扩大了检测能力并改进了菌株特异性中和反应的评估。结论 这种基于假型的微量中和试验是传统活病毒中和试验的一种安全、灵活且可扩展的替代方法。通过在 BSL-2 条件下启用高通量 狂犬病 血清学,该平台支持 疫苗 评估和血清流行病学 监测,特别是在 BSL-3 基础设施有限的环境中。

Peer Review Report For: Development and characterization of pseudotype-based microneutralization assay to assess functionality of anti-rabies protein G antibodies [version 1; peer review: 2 approved with reservations]

Authors: Antonelli R#, Vezzani G#, Guerrini N, Giovagnoli V, Dapporto F, Iturriza M, Loomis R, Montomoli E, Rossi O, Molesti E
Original abstract (English)
Background Rabies virus (RABV) is a neurotropic zoonotic pathogen responsible for an estimated 60,000 human deaths annually. Despite widespread vaccination programs, rabies remains a substantial public health burden, particularly in regions with limited access to post-exposure prophylaxis (PEP). Standard serological assays for evaluating rabies vaccine immunogenicity, including the Rapid Fluorescent Focus Inhibition Test (RFFIT) and the Fluorescent Antibody Virus Neutralization (FAVN) test, require live virus handling under biosafety level 3 (BSL-3) conditions, limiting their scalability and broader implementation. Methods We developed and characterized a pseudotype-based microneutralization assay using a lentiviral HIV-1 backbone to generate pseudotyped viral particles expressing glycoproteins from three antigenically distinct rabies virus strains: CTN-1 V5, CVS-11, and Pasteur. Neutralization assays were performed under BSL-2 conditions, and assay performance was evaluated in terms of sensitivity, specificity, and suitability for high-throughput applications. Results The pseudotype-based assay enabled sensitive and reproducible detection of rabies virus–neutralizing antibodies across the three glycoprotein variants. The platform demonstrated robust performance under BSL-2 containment, supporting efficient and scalable testing. Inclusion of antigenically distinct strains broadened detection capacity and improved the assessment of strain-specific neutralizing responses. Conclusions This pseudotype-based microneutralization assay represents a safe, flexible, and scalable alternative to conventional live-virus neutralization tests. By enabling high-throughput rabies serology under BSL-2 conditions, this platform supports vaccine evaluation and seroepidemiological surveillance, particularly in settings where BSL-3 infrastructure is limited.

Main results

Interpretation and novelty

雄性小鼠下丘脑室旁核促肾上腺皮质激素释放激素神经元的全脑单突触输入和输出。

Whole-Brain Monosynaptic Inputs and Outputs of Corticotropin-Releasing Hormone Neurons of the Paraventricular Nucleus of the Hypothalamus in Male Mice.
作者： Ziteng Yue, Danyang Zhao, Xinxin Chen, Yousheng Zhang, Heng Zhang, Yinchang Wang, Yuhang Liu, Yuxin Yang, Can Geng, Yimeng Song
摘要中文翻译目的 下丘脑室旁核(PVN)是整合神经内分泌、自主神经和行为反应的中枢。PVN 中的促肾上腺皮质激素释放激素 (CRH) 神经元 (PVN CRH ) 在应激反应、焦虑和动机中发挥着关键作用。然而,PVN CRH 神经元的全脑单突触输入输出架构仍未完全定义,限制了基于电路的功能解剖。方法使用 Cre 依赖性修饰的 狂犬病病毒 进行逆行单突触追踪,并使用腺相关病毒进行 CRH-Cre 小鼠的顺行追踪,我们系统地绘制了 PVN CRH 神经元的全脑输入和输出。结果 PVN CRH 神经元接收来自 55 个大脑区域的直接输入,其中主要贡献来自间脑区域和总体同侧偏差 (61.4%)。值得注意的是,高度特异性的输入源自腹侧海马 CA1 和伏隔核核心。PVN CRH 神经元还在整个大脑中发出广泛的轴突投射。这些输出汇聚到多个电路模块上,并密集投射到与情绪相关的核团和自主调节中心。结论本研究建立了一个全面的PVN CRH神经元全脑单突触输入输出图谱。由此产生的神经解剖学框架描绘了它们与压力、情绪、自主神经和呼吸相关回路的广泛连接,为未来对压力相关精神疾病和系统性自主神经失调的回路水平研究提供了结构基础。

问题与背景

该研究在使用CRH-Cre小鼠的雄性小鼠中进行,以系统地绘制PVN CRH神经元的全脑输入和输出。研究时间从2026-09-01开始,采用了一种全面的方法来研究神经连接性。

研究人员在 CRH-Cre 小鼠中使用 Cre 依赖性修饰的 狂犬病病毒 进行逆行单突触追踪,并使用腺相关病毒进行顺行追踪,以绘制 PVN CRH 神经元的全脑输入和输出图。

Whole-Brain Monosynaptic Inputs and Outputs of Corticotropin-Releasing Hormone Neurons of the Paraventricular Nucleus of the Hypothalamus in Male Mice.

Authors: Ziteng Yue, Danyang Zhao, Xinxin Chen, Yousheng Zhang, Heng Zhang, Yinchang Wang, Yuhang Liu, Yuxin Yang, Can Geng, Yimeng Song
Original abstract (English)
Objective The paraventricular nucleus of the hypothalamus (PVN) is a central hub integrating neuroendocrine, autonomic, and behavioral responses. Corticotropin-releasing hormone (CRH) neurons in the PVN (PVN CRH ) play a pivotal role in stress responses, anxiety, and motivation. However, the brain-wide monosynaptic input-output architecture of PVN CRH neurons remains incompletely defined, limiting circuit-based functional dissection. Methods Using Cre-dependent modified rabies virus for retrograde monosynaptic tracing and adeno-associated viruses for anterograde tracing in CRH-Cre mice, we systematically mapped the whole-brain inputs and outputs of PVN CRH neurons. Results PVN CRH neurons received direct inputs from 55 brain regions, with a predominant contribution from diencephalon areas and an overall ipsilateral bias (61.4%). Notably, highly specific inputs originated from the ventral hippocampal CA1 and nucleus accumbens core. PVN CRH neurons also sent widespread axonal projections throughout the brain. These outputs converge onto multiple circuit modules, with dense projections to emotion-related nuclei and autonomic regulatory centers. Conclusion This study establishes a comprehensive whole-brain monosynaptic input-output atlas of PVN CRH neurons. The resultant neuroanatomical framework delineates their extensive connectivity with stress-, emotion-, autonomic-, and respiration-related circuits, providing a structural basis for future circuit-level investigations into stress-related psychiatric disorders and systemic autonomic dysregulation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to define the brain-wide monosynaptic input-output architecture of PVN CRH neurons, which are pivotal in stress responses, anxiety, and motivation. This knowledge gap limits circuit-based functional dissection.
Design and population
The study was conducted in male mice using CRH-Cre mice to systematically map the whole-brain inputs and outputs of PVN CRH neurons. The research spanned from 2026-09-01 and involved a comprehensive approach to neural connectivity.
Core methods
The researchers employed Cre-dependent modified rabies virus for retrograde monosynaptic tracing and adeno-associated viruses for anterograde tracing in CRH-Cre mice to map the whole-brain inputs and outputs of PVN CRH neurons.
Main results
PVN CRH neurons received direct inputs from 55 brain regions, with a predominant contribution from diencephalon areas and an overall ipsilateral bias (61.4%). Highly specific inputs originated from the ventral hippocampal CA1 and nucleus accumbens core.
Interpretation and novelty
The study provides a structural basis for future circuit-level investigations into stress-related psychiatric disorders and systemic autonomic dysregulation by delineating the extensive connectivity of PVN CRH neurons.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings offer insights into the neural circuits involved in stress, emotion, and autonomic regulation, which could inform future research on stress-related psychiatric disorders and systemic autonomic dysregulation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to male mice, and the evidence is based on a comprehensive mapping approach, which provides a high confidence assessment of the connectivity patterns of PVN CRH neurons.

重建瑞士狂犬病的分子流行病学,从暴发到消除(1967-1997)

Reconstructing the molecular epidemiology of rabies in Switzerland from outbreak to elimination (1967–1997)
作者： Farzane Shams, Claudia Bachofen, Alex Popinga, Monika Hilbe, Michele Wyler, Torsten Seuberlich
摘要中文翻译摘要 狂犬病病毒 (RABV) 是一种致命的人畜共患病原体,尽管经过长期的 疫苗接种 努力,该病原体在世界某些地区仍然流行。我们对瑞士森林 狂犬病 流行病(1967-1997 年)进行了全面的基因组和进化分析,并提出了一个描述 RABV 从出现到消除的动态的分子流行病学框架,与追求消除野生动物 狂犬病 的国家相关。我们从三十年来在瑞士 17 个州和 13 个 宿主 物种收集的 88 个 RABV 阳性大脑样本中生成了几乎完整的 基因组 序列。使用 Illumina 测序,以高覆盖度回收了 76 个 基因组,从而实现了稳健的单核苷酸多态性 (SNP) 和分子钟分析。基于瑞士完整 基因组 和糖蛋白序列(包括其他欧洲毒株)的系统发育重建确定了三个时间结构的 RABV 谱系——红色(1967-1976)、蓝色(1976-1988)和绿色(1984-1996)——每个谱系都与来自邻国的独立引入一致。进化分析表明缓慢的取代率和主要的纯化选择,以及有限的适应性氨基酸变化。然而,在流行病学高峰期间出现了四个 突变,这表明健身效果可能不大。我们没有发现与跨物种 跨物种溢出 相关的主要基因组变化的证据。四种同义变异在食肉动物 宿主 中代表性不足;然而,更大的数据集可能为 宿主 相关的遗传差异提供更有力的证据。遗传分化显示出强烈的时间信号和与地理距离的中度相关性,与限制RABV传播的自然和疫苗接种诱导的障碍一致,与已知的流行病生态和控制历史一致。这些发现为理解森林狂犬病的出现和消除提供了一个基因组框架,并可能为实现和维持世界卫生组织(WHO)由狗介导的狂犬病造成的人类零死亡的目标提供策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
尽管 疫苗接种 做出了长期努力,但 狂犬病病毒 (RABV) 在世界某些地区仍然流行。本研究旨在了解瑞士狂犬病的分子流行病学从暴发到消除。
设计与对象
对 1967 年至 1997 年间瑞士森林 狂犬病 流行病进行了全面的基因组和进化分析。该研究包括三十年来从瑞士 17 个州和 13 个 宿主 物种收集的 88 个 RABV 阳性大脑样本。
核心方法
使用 Illumina 测序从 88 个 RABV 阳性大脑样本中生成几乎完整的 基因组 序列。76 基因组 以高覆盖率恢复,从而实现稳健的单核苷酸多态性 (SNP) 和分子钟分析。
主要结果
系统发育重建确定了三个时间结构的RABV谱系——红色(1967-1976)、蓝色(1976-1988)和绿色(1984-1996)——每个谱系都与从邻国独立引入的情况一致。遗传分化表现出强烈的时间信号和与地理距离的中等相关性。
解释与创新
这些发现为理解森林狂犬病的出现和消除提供了一个基因组框架,为RABV传播的动态和狂犬病努力的影响提供了见解。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可以通过强调自然和 狂犬病 诱导的障碍在限制 RABV 传播方面的作用,为实现和维持世界卫生组织 (WHO) 狗介导的 狂犬病 导致人类零死亡的目标提供策略依据。
局限与证据强度
该研究没有发现与跨物种 跨物种溢出 相关的主要基因组变化的证据,并且四种同义变异在食肉动物 宿主 中代表性不足。然而,更大的数据集可能为 宿主 相关的遗传差异提供更有力的证据。

Reconstructing the molecular epidemiology of rabies in Switzerland from outbreak to elimination (1967–1997)

Authors: Farzane Shams, Claudia Bachofen, Alex Popinga, Monika Hilbe, Michele Wyler, Torsten Seuberlich
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Rabies virus (RABV) is a lethal zoonotic pathogen that remains endemic in some parts of the world despite long-standing vaccination efforts. We present a comprehensive genomic and evolutionary analysis of the sylvatic rabies epidemic in Switzerland (1967–1997) and propose a molecular epidemiological framework describing RABV dynamics from emergence to elimination, with relevance for countries pursuing wildlife rabies elimination. We generated almost whole-genome sequences from 88 RABV-positive brain samples collected across 17 Swiss cantons and 13 host species over three decades. Using Illumina sequencing, 76 genomes were recovered at high coverage, enabling robust Single Nucleotide Polymorphism (SNP) and molecular clock analyses. Phylogenetic reconstruction based on Swiss whole genomes and glycoprotein sequences including additional European strains identified three temporally structured RABV lineages—red (1967–1976), blue (1976–1988), and green (1984–1996)—each consistent with independent introductions from neighbouring countries. Evolutionary analyses indicated a slow substitution rate and predominant purifying selection, with limited adaptive amino-acid change. However, four mutations emerged during the epidemiological peak, suggesting possible modest fitness effects. We found no evidence of major genomic changes associated with cross-species spillovers. Four synonymous variants were underrepresented in carnivore hosts; however, larger datasets may provide stronger evidence for host-associated genetic differences. Genetic divergence showed a strong temporal signal and moderate correlation with geographic distance, consistent with natural and vaccination-induced barriers limiting RABV dispersal, in agreement with the known ecology and control history of the epidemic. These findings provide a genomic framework for understanding sylvatic rabies emergence and elimination and may inform strategies to achieve and sustain the World Health Organization (WHO) goal of zero human deaths from dog-mediated rabies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Rabies virus (RABV) remains endemic in some parts of the world despite long-standing vaccination efforts. This study aimed to understand the molecular epidemiology of rabies in Switzerland from outbreak to elimination.
Design and population
A comprehensive genomic and evolutionary analysis was conducted on the sylvatic rabies epidemic in Switzerland between 1967 and 1997. The study included 88 RABV-positive brain samples collected across 17 Swiss cantons and 13 host species over three decades.
Core methods
Almost whole-genome sequences were generated from 88 RABV-positive brain samples using Illumina sequencing. 76 genomes were recovered at high coverage, enabling robust Single Nucleotide Polymorphism (SNP) and molecular clock analyses.
Main results
Phylogenetic reconstruction identified three temporally structured RABV lineages—red (1967–1976), blue (1976–1988), and green (1984–1996)—each consistent with independent introductions from neighboring countries. Genetic divergence showed a strong temporal signal and moderate correlation with geographic distance.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings provide a genomic framework for understanding sylvatic rabies emergence and elimination, offering insights into the dynamics of RABV spread and the impact of vaccination efforts.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results may inform strategies to achieve and sustain the World Health Organization (WHO) goal of zero human deaths from dog-mediated rabies by highlighting the role of natural and vaccination-induced barriers in limiting RABV dispersal.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study found no evidence of major genomic changes associated with cross-species spillovers, and four synonymous variants were underrepresented in carnivore hosts. However, larger datasets may provide stronger evidence for host-associated genetic differences.

影响人类的与狗相关的疾病:中国和赞比亚案例研究中人畜共患感染的综合调查

Dog related diseases affecting humans: a comprehensive survey of zoonotic infections from case studies in China and Zambia
作者： Tianren Shen
摘要中文翻译狂犬病 是一种高度致命、可传播的人畜共患疾病,每年在 150 多个国家造成约 59,000 人死亡,其中 95% 以上的病例发生在亚洲和非洲,给公共卫生造成巨大负担。它仍然是中国和赞比亚最严重的狗介导的人畜共患病之一。在赞比亚,周期性的暴发是由家犬驱动的,作为主要的储存宿主,使农村儿童面临特别高的风险,而中国内陆省份继续经历以狗为媒介的狂犬病 发病率。除了 狂犬病 之外,血源性病原体等新兴威胁缺乏系统的量化。这项研究提供了两国之间的直接流行病学比较,以便为有针对性的干预措施提供信息。该研究采用混合方法,包括基于知识、态度和实践(KAP)框架的配额抽样、横断面观察调查和焦点小组讨论(n = 39),对海南(n = 2,388)和曼布韦(n = 433)的居民进行调查。虽然大多数参与者对 狂犬病 持积极态度,但在知识和实践方面都出现了巨大差距。常见缺陷包括(1)缺乏对 狂犬病 无法治愈的认识,(2)对 狂犬病 的 传播 通路了解不足,以及(3)对看似健康的狗所带来的风险了解有限。特别是在曼布韦,还发现了其他问题:(4) 狗登记和 疫苗接种 覆盖率低,以及 (5) 养狗自由漫游的普遍做法。为了控制并最终消除狂犬病,我们建议针对具体情况的策略:(1)在人类住区和野生动物保护区之间执行严格的界限;(二)投资农村基础设施;(3) 加强社区参与;(4) 采用“同一个健康”方法。在 Mambwe 地区,从国家公园边界附近农民拥有的 277 只家养狗身上采集了血液样本。使用牛津纳米孔技术的宏基因组测序揭示了六种致病微生物物种或亚种:扁平无形体、犬埃利希体、多杀性巴斯德氏菌、刚果锥虫 IL3000、布氏锥虫和布氏锥虫。这些病原体构成重大的跨物种 传播 和人畜共患 跨物种溢出 风险,威胁人类健康、牲畜生产力和野生动物保护。值得注意的是,首次在赞比亚狗身上检测到多杀性巴氏杆菌。这些发现提供了对家犬在这一独特农业系统中的作用的详细见解,并为未来的 One Health 管理战略和政策制定奠定了科学基础。预计它们将为亚洲和非洲 狂犬病 政策和有针对性的控制措施的完善提供信息,从而支持与狗相关的疾病的预防。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
狂犬病 是一种高度致命、可传播的人畜共患疾病,每年在 150 多个国家造成约 59,000 人死亡,其中 95% 以上的病例发生在亚洲和非洲,给公共卫生造成巨大负担。
设计与对象
该研究采用混合方法,包括基于知识、态度和实践(KAP)框架的配额抽样、横断面观察调查和焦点小组讨论(n = 39),对海南(n = 2,388)和曼布韦(n = 433)的居民进行调查。
核心方法
在姆邦贝区,从居住在国家公园边界附近的农民所拥有的277只家犬中采集了血液样本。
主要结果
值得注意的是,Pasteurella multocida首次在赞比亚犬中被检测到。
解释与创新
这些发现提供了对家犬在这一独特农业系统中的作用的详细见解,并为未来的 One Health 管理战略和政策制定奠定了科学基础。

这些病原体构成重大的跨物种 传播 和人畜共患 跨物种溢出 风险,威胁人类健康、牲畜生产力和野生动物保护。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据未报告具体限制或证据强度评估。

Dog related diseases affecting humans: a comprehensive survey of zoonotic infections from case studies in China and Zambia

Authors: Tianren Shen
Original abstract (English)
Rabies is a highly fatal and transmissible zoonotic disease, causing approximately 59,000 human deaths each year across more than 150 countries, with over 95% of cases occurring in Asia and Africa and imposing a substantial public health burden. It remains one of the most serious dog-mediated zoonoses in both China and Zambia. In Zambia, periodic outbreaks are driven by domestic dogs as the principal reservoir, placing rural children at especially high risk, while inland provinces of China continue to experience dog-mediated rabies incidence. Beyond rabies, emerging threats such as blood-borne pathogens lack systematic quantification. This study provides a direct epidemiological comparison between the two countries to inform targeted interventions. The study employed mixed methods, including a quota-sampled, cross-sectional observational survey based on the Knowledge, Attitudes, and Practices (KAP) framework and focus group discussions (n = 39), surveying residents in Hainan (n = 2,388) and Mambwe (n = 433). While most participants held positive attitudes toward rabies, significant gaps emerged in both knowledge and practice. Common deficits included (1) a lack of awareness that rabies is incurable, (2) insufficient knowledge regarding the transmission pathways of rabies, and (3) limited understanding of the risks posed by seemingly healthy dogs. In Mambwe specifically, additional issues were identified: (4) low coverage of dog registration and vaccination and (5) widespread practices of free-roaming dog ownership. To control and ultimately eliminate rabies, we recommend context‐specific strategies: (1) enforcing strict boundaries between human settlements and wildlife conservation areas; (2) investing in rural infrastructure; (3) enhancing community engagement; and (4) adopting One Health approach. In Mambwe District, blood samples were collected from 277 domestic dogs owned by farmers living adjacent to the national park boundary. Metagenomic sequencing using Oxford Nanopore Technologies revealed six pathogenic microbial species or subspecies: Anaplasma platys, Ehrlichia canis, Pasteurella multocida, Trypanosoma congolense IL3000, Trypanosoma brucei, and Trypanosoma brucei brucei. These pathogens pose significant cross‐species transmission and zoonotic spillover risks, threatening human health, livestock productivity, and wildlife conservation. Notably, Pasteurella multocida was detected in Zambian dogs for the first time. These findings provide detailed insight into the role of domestic dogs within this distinctive agricultural system and establish a scientific foundation for future One Health management strategies and policy development. They are expected to inform the refinement of rabies policies and targeted control measures in Asia and Africa, thereby supporting the prevention of dog‐associated diseases.

Question and background
Rabies is a highly fatal and transmissible zoonotic disease, causing approximately 59,000 human deaths each year across more than 150 countries, with over 95% of cases occurring in Asia and Africa and imposing a substantial public health burden.
Design and population
The study employed mixed methods, including a quota-sampled, cross-sectional observational survey based on the Knowledge, Attitudes, and Practices (KAP) framework and focus group discussions (n = 39), surveying residents in Hainan (n = 2,388) and Mambwe (n = 433).
Core methods
In Mambwe District, blood samples were collected from 277 domestic dogs owned by farmers living adjacent to the national park boundary.
Main results
Notably, Pasteurella multocida was detected in Zambian dogs for the first time.
Interpretation and novelty

Scientific and public-health significance
These pathogens pose significant cross‐species transmission and zoonotic spillover risks, threatening human health, livestock productivity, and wildlife conservation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations or evidence strength assessment.

关于狂犬病的公众意识和知识:基于调查的分析

Public awareness and knowledge on rabies: a survey-based analysis
作者： Twinkle Gloria Daniel, Shweta Pattnaik, Priyanka Tiwari, Ankit Nand, Eunice Solomon, Yogita Rahangdale
摘要中文翻译背景:狂犬病 是一种致命但可预防的病毒性疾病,会攻击中枢神经系统,在 150 多个国家流行。尽管 疫苗 和 治疗 取得了进展,但 狂犬病 仍然是一种被忽视的热带病。因此,本研究旨在评估普通人群对 狂犬病 的认知度和知识。方法:采用定量描述性横断面调查研究设计来进行研究。使用来自印度各地社交媒体平台的随机抽样技术,从普通人群中选取了 567 名参与者作为研究样本。结果:研究显示,总体而言,68.43%的参与者了解狂犬病的传播,48.68%的参与者了解狂犬病的症状,69.49%的参与者了解狂犬病的预防措施。对 狂犬病 的 传播、狂犬病 的症状和 狂犬病 的预防措施的认识与参与者的任何人口统计学变量之间没有发现显着关联 结论:研究得出结论,一般人群的知识水平为轻度至中度。因此,对各种 狂犬病 疾病及其预防的正确认识有助于降低普通人群的此类风险。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
狂犬病 是一种致命但可预防的病毒性 CNS 疾病,在 150 多个国家流行,尽管 疫苗 和 治疗 取得了进展,但它仍然是一种被忽视的热带疾病,本研究旨在评估普通人群对 狂犬病 的认识和知识。
设计与对象
通过社交媒体平台使用随机抽样技术在印度各地进行了一项定量描述性横断面调查,从普通人群中招募了 567 名参与者。
核心方法
定量描述性横断面调查设计是通过社交媒体平台分发的在线调查问卷,从印度各地的普通人群中随机抽取了 567 名参与者。
主要结果
在 567 名参与者中,68.43% 的人了解 狂犬病 传播,48.68% 的人了解 狂犬病 的症状,69.49% 的人知道预防措施,并且在对 狂犬病 的认知、症状或预防措施与任何人口统计变量之间没有发现显着关联。
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,在印度接受调查的普通人群中,仅存在轻度至中度的 狂犬病 相关知识,这凸显了持续存在的知识差距,尽管基本的 传播 意识超过了预防和症状知识。
科研与公卫意义
通过有针对性的教育活动提高公众对 狂犬病 疾病和预防的认识,有助于降低印度等流行国家普通人群中与 狂犬病 相关的风险。
局限与证据强度
局限性包括依赖于随机抽样的单一社交媒体平台、限制来自一个国家的参与者、缺乏人口统计学关联,以及仅抽象的证据基础妨碍了对问卷有效性的评估,从而对调查结果产生了中等的总体信心。

Public awareness and knowledge on rabies: a survey-based analysis

Authors: Twinkle Gloria Daniel, Shweta Pattnaik, Priyanka Tiwari, Ankit Nand, Eunice Solomon, Yogita Rahangdale
Original abstract (English)
Background: Rabies is a fatal yet preventable viral disease that attacks the central nervous system and is endemic in more than 150 countries. Despite advancements in vaccines and treatments, rabies remains a neglected tropical disease. Therefore, present study aims to assess the awareness and knowledge about rabies among general population. Methods: Quantitative descriptive cross-sectional survey research design was used to conduct the study. A sample of 567 participants from general population was selected for the study using random sampling technique from social media platform across India. Results: Study revealed that overall, 68.43% participants were aware about transmission of rabies, 48.68% participants were knowledgeable about symptoms of rabies and 69.49% participants had knowledge about preventive measures about rabies. There was no significant association found between awareness regarding transmission of rabies, symptoms of rabies and preventive measures of rabies with any of demographic variables of the participants Conclusions: Study concluded that there was a mild to moderate level of knowledge among general population. Hence, proper awareness regarding various rabies diseases and its prevention can help in reducing such risk among general population.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Rabies is a fatal yet preventable viral CNS disease endemic in more than 150 countries and remains a neglected tropical disease despite vaccine and treatment advances, and the present study aimed to assess awareness and knowledge about rabies among the general population.
Design and population
A quantitative descriptive cross-sectional survey was conducted across India using a random sampling technique via a social media platform, recruiting 567 participants from the general population.
Core methods
A quantitative descriptive cross-sectional survey design was administered as an online questionnaire distributed through a social media platform, drawing a random sample of 567 participants from the general population across India.
Main results
Among 567 participants, 68.43% were aware of rabies transmission, 48.68% were knowledgeable about rabies symptoms, and 69.49% knew preventive measures, and no significant association was found between awareness of transmission, symptoms or preventive measures and any demographic variable.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that only a mild to moderate level of rabies-related knowledge existed among the surveyed general population in India, highlighting persistent knowledge gaps even though basic transmission awareness exceeded preventive and symptom knowledge.
Scientific and public-health significance
Improving public awareness of rabies disease and prevention through targeted education campaigns could help reduce rabies-related risk among the general population in endemic countries such as India.
Limitations and evidence strength
Limitations include reliance on a single social-media platform with random sampling, restriction to participants from one country, absence of demographic associations, and an abstract-only evidence base that precludes assessment of questionnaire validity, yielding a moderate overall confidence in the findings.

接受抗 狂犬病 疫苗 免疫的患者出现疑似 狂犬病 病例

Suspected case of rabies in a patient immunized with anti-rabies vaccine
作者： Vrunda T. Parikh, Sapna D. Gupta, Supriya D. Malhotra
摘要中文翻译狂犬病 仍然是一个重大的公共卫生威胁,特别是在印度这样的发展中国家,流浪狗占人类病例的 99%。一旦出现症状,这种疾病几乎总是致命的,几天之内就会死亡。尽管使用 WHO 推荐的 疫苗 预治疗和 暴露后预防 (PEP) 非常有效,但偶尔出现 疫苗 失败等挑战仍然存在。在印度,狂犬病 每年导致约 20,000 人死亡。该病例强调了尽管接受 PEP 仍出现的疑似 狂犬病 感染,探讨了失败的潜在原因。这个案例强调了深入了解可能导致疫苗无效的因素的必要性。它还强调了施用 狂犬病 免疫球蛋白 (RIG)、适当的伤口护理和遵循既定指南的关键作用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
狂犬病 仍然是一个重大的公共卫生威胁,特别是在印度这样的发展中国家,流浪狗占人类病例的 99%。
设计与对象
本案例研究描述了一名接受过 WHO 推荐的 疫苗 预治疗和 暴露后预防 (PEP) 治疗的患者疑似 狂犬病 感染,强调了 疫苗 失败的潜在原因。
核心方法
尽管使用 WHO 推荐的 疫苗 预治疗和 暴露后预防 (PEP) 非常有效,但偶尔出现 疫苗 失败等挑战仍然存在。
主要结果
该病例强调了尽管接受 PEP 仍出现的疑似 狂犬病 感染,探讨了失败的潜在原因。
解释与创新
这个案例强调了深入了解可能导致疫苗无效的因素的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
它还强调了施用 狂犬病 免疫球蛋白 (RIG)、适当的伤口护理和遵循既定指南的关键作用。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据未报告具体限制或证据强度评估。

Suspected case of rabies in a patient immunized with anti-rabies vaccine

Authors: Vrunda T. Parikh, Sapna D. Gupta, Supriya D. Malhotra
Original abstract (English)
Rabies remains a major public health threat, especially in developing countries like India, where stray dogs account for 99% of human cases. The disease is almost always fatal once symptoms appear, with death occurring within days. Despite highly effective pre- and post-exposure prophylaxis (PEP) with WHO-recommended vaccines, challenges like occasional vaccine failure persist. In India, rabies causes approximately 20,000 deaths annually. This case highlights a suspected rabies infection that developed despite receiving PEP, exploring potential reasons for the failure. This case emphasizes the necessity for a deeper understanding of factors that may lead to vaccine ineffectiveness. It also underscores the critical role of administering rabies immunoglobulin (RIG), proper wound care and following established guidelines.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Rabies remains a major public health threat, especially in developing countries like India, where stray dogs account for 99% of human cases.
Design and population
This case study describes a suspected rabies infection in a patient who had received pre- and post-exposure prophylaxis (PEP) with WHO-recommended vaccines, highlighting potential reasons for vaccine failure.
Core methods
Despite highly effective pre- and post-exposure prophylaxis (PEP) with WHO-recommended vaccines, challenges like occasional vaccine failure persist.
Main results
This case highlights a suspected rabies infection that developed despite receiving PEP, exploring potential reasons for the failure.
Interpretation and novelty
This case emphasizes the necessity for a deeper understanding of factors that may lead to vaccine ineffectiveness.
Scientific and public-health significance
It also underscores the critical role of administering rabies immunoglobulin (RIG), proper wound care and following established guidelines.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations or evidence strength assessment.

弥合 狂犬病 的知识差距:巴基斯坦全科医生意识和临床管理的横断面研究

Bridging the Knowledge Gap on Rabies: A Cross-Sectional Study of General Practitioners’ Awareness and Clinical Management in Pakistan
作者： Naveed Mansoori, Zohaib ur Rehman Mufti, Rashid Jamal, Imran Bakar, Rabia Yasmin, Syed Muhammad Mubeen
摘要中文翻译目的:本研究旨在评估巴基斯坦全科医生对 狂犬病 及其管理的了解。方法:这项横断面研究于 2023 年 3 月至 12 月在巴基斯坦不同城市的公立和私人诊所或医院执业的全科医生中进行。使用非概率方便抽样技术,在全科医生中进行了预先测试的结构化调查问卷,以评估有关 狂犬病 及其管理的知识和实践。获得所有变量的频率和百分比的描述性统计数据。连续变量以平均值±标准差表示。独立 t 检验用于比较平均知识和实践得分及其与人口统计变量的关联。数据采用SPSS 24版进行分析。P值<0.05被认为有统计学意义。结果:500名全科医生中,258名(51.6%)来自公立医院,108名(21.6%)拥有10年以上经验。参与者的总体平均年龄为 36.3 ± 7.8 岁。全科医生(89.4%)正确了解病原体,(49.4%)正确了解 媒介 或 狂犬病。超过一半的全科医生 (53.4%) 正确回答,可以使用细胞培养物 疫苗 或神经组织 疫苗 来实现 狂犬病 预防,并且 (66%) 了解有关抗 狂犬病 疫苗接种 时间表的知识。关于狗咬伤伤口的处理(85.4%)有适当的冲洗知识和(27.4%)有缝合知识。总体而言(52%)全科医生认识到需要对被狗咬伤的患者使用抗生素和破伤风抗毒素。从统计上看,知识和实践得分与社会人口特征之间没有显着关联。结论:这项研究表明,巴基斯坦全科医生对 狂犬病 及其管理的了解不足。应经常为全科医生举办有关狗咬伤患者的认识和管理的会议,以控制和减少与人类 狂犬病 相关的死亡。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估巴基斯坦全科医生对 狂犬病 及其管理的了解。
设计与对象
这项横断面研究于 2023 年 3 月至 12 月在巴基斯坦不同城市的公立和私人诊所或医院执业的全科医生中进行。
核心方法
使用非概率方便抽样技术,在全科医生中进行了预先测试的结构化调查问卷,以评估有关 狂犬病 及其管理的知识和实践。
主要结果
500名全科医生中,258名(51.6%)来自公立医院,108名(21.6%)拥有10年以上经验。
解释与创新
从统计上看,知识和实践得分与社会人口特征之间没有观察到显着的关联。
科研与公卫意义
这项研究表明巴基斯坦全科医生对 狂犬病 及其管理的了解不足。
局限与证据强度
P值<0.05被认为具有统计学意义。

Bridging the Knowledge Gap on Rabies: A Cross-Sectional Study of General Practitioners’ Awareness and Clinical Management in Pakistan

Authors: Naveed Mansoori, Zohaib ur Rehman Mufti, Rashid Jamal, Imran Bakar, Rabia Yasmin, Syed Muhammad Mubeen
Original abstract (English)
Objective: This study aimed to assess the knowledge regarding rabies and its management among general practitioners in Pakistan.Methods: This cross-sectional study was conducted from March to December 2023 among general practitioners practicing in public and private clinics or hospitals in different cities of Pakistan. Using non-probability convenient sampling technique, a pre-tested structured questionnaires was administered among GPs to assess the knowledge and practices regarding rabies and its management. Descriptive statistics for frequency and percentage were acquired for all variables. Continuous variables are presented as mean ± SD. Independent t-test was used to compare mean knowledge and practice score and its association with demographic variables. Data was analyzed by using SPSS version 24. A P value <0.05 was considered statistically significant.Results: Out of total 500 general practitioners, 258 (51.6%) from public hospitals, and 108 (21.6%) had more than 10 years of experience. The overall mean age of the participants was 36.3 ± 7.8 years. General practitioners, (89.4%) correctly knew about the causative agent and (49.4%) about the vector of rabies. More than half of the general practitioners (53.4%) responded correctly that rabies prevention can be achieved using either cell culture vaccines or nervous tissue vaccines and (66%)had knowledge regarding anti-rabies vaccination schedule. Regarding management of dog bite wound (85.4%) had appropriate knowledge about irrigation and (27.4%) about suturing. Collectively (52%) general practitioners recognized the need to administer antibiotics along with tetanus antitoxin to patients with dog bite. Statistically no significant association was observed between knowledge and practice scores with socio-demographic characteristics.Conclusion: This study revealed that the knowledge of rabies and its management was inadequate among general practitioners of Pakistan. Frequent sessions on awareness and management of dog bite patients should be held for general practitioners to control and reduce the incidence of human rabies related deaths.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to assess the knowledge regarding rabies and its management among general practitioners in Pakistan.
Design and population
This cross-sectional study was conducted from March to December 2023 among general practitioners practicing in public and private clinics or hospitals in different cities of Pakistan.
Core methods
Using non-probability convenient sampling technique, a pre-tested structured questionnaires was administered among GPs to assess the knowledge and practices regarding rabies and its management.
Main results
Out of total 500 general practitioners, 258 (51.6%) from public hospitals, and 108 (21.6%) had more than 10 years of experience.
Interpretation and novelty
Statistically no significant association was observed between knowledge and practice scores with socio-demographic characteristics.
Scientific and public-health significance
This study revealed that the knowledge of rabies and its management was inadequate among general practitioners of Pakistan.
Limitations and evidence strength
A P value <0.05 was considered statistically significant.

用于神经回路高通量重建的条码 狂犬病 原位连接组学

Barcoded Rabies In Situ Connectomics for high-throughput reconstruction of neural circuits
作者： Alexander Becalick, Antonin Blot, Molly Strom, Petr Znamenskiy
摘要中文翻译摘要 要从机制上理解神经元网络如何进行神经计算,需要了解其潜在的突触连接性。然而,当前重建突触连接的方法非常费力,并且通常仅限于映射附近神经元之间的连接。在这里,我们开发了条形码 狂犬病 原位连接组学 (BRISC),它使用携带随机分子条形码的 狂犬病病毒 并行映射数百个神经元的局部和远程单突触输入。为了确保大多数突触后起始神经元都用不同的条形码序列进行唯一标记,我们生成了具有足够多样性的 狂犬病病毒 文库来标记 > 1000 个神经元,并通过限制起始神经元的密度来控制起始神经元之间条形码 传播 的概率。我们应用 BRISC 来绘制小鼠初级视觉皮层中单个神经元的输入图。我们原位读出了 狂犬病 感染的神经元中病毒条形码的表达,保留了空间信息。然后,我们匹配起始神经元和突触前神经元之间的条形码序列,映射 385 个神经元的输入并识别 7,814 个假定的突触连接,揭示特定于层和细胞类型的局部连接规则以及远程输入的拓扑组织。这些结果表明,BRISC 可以同时解析数百个神经元的突触连接,从而能够以前所未有的规模重建神经回路。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决对更有效的方法来映射神经回路中突触连接的需求,因为当前的技术非常费力并且仅限于局部连接。背景强调了理解神经网络如何通过其突触架构执行计算的重要性。
设计与对象
该研究开发了一种名为条形码 狂犬病 原位连接组学 (BRISC) 的新方法,用于绘制小鼠初级视觉皮层神经元的局部和远程单突触输入图。该方法适用于该特定大脑区域的单个神经元。
核心方法
BRISC 利用带有随机分子条形码的 狂犬病病毒 来标记神经元,从而可以同时映射突触连接。该技术涉及读取受感染神经元中的病毒条形码表达以及匹配起始神经元和突触前神经元之间的序列以确定连接性。
主要结果
该方法成功映射了 385 个神经元的输入,并识别了 7,814 个假定的突触连接,揭示了特定于层和细胞类型的局部连接规则以及远程输入的拓扑组织。
解释与创新
作者将这些结果解释为证明了 BRISC 能够以前所未有的规模解决突触连接问题,从而在神经回路重建方面取得了重大进展。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现对神经科学研究具有重要意义,可以对神经回路及其功能组织进行更全面的研究,这可以为未来对神经计算和疾病机制的研究提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于小鼠初级视觉皮层,并未涉及更广泛的应用或其他神经区域。证据是强有力的,因为它直接报告了 BRISC 在高精度映射突触连接方面的成功应用。

Barcoded Rabies In Situ Connectomics for high-throughput reconstruction of neural circuits

Authors: Alexander Becalick, Antonin Blot, Molly Strom, Petr Znamenskiy
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Mechanistic understanding of how networks of neurons carry out neural computations requires knowledge of their underlying synaptic connectivity. However, current methods for reconstructing synaptic connections are extremely laborious and typically limited to mapping connections between nearby neurons. Here we developed Barcoded Rabies In Situ Connectomics (BRISC), which uses rabies viruses carrying random molecular barcodes to map both local and long-range monosynaptic inputs of hundreds of neurons in parallel. To ensure that the majority of post-synaptic starter neurons are uniquely labeled with distinct barcode sequences, we generated libraries of rabies viruses with sufficient diversity to label >1000 neurons and controlled the probability of barcode transmission between starter neurons by limiting their density. We applied BRISC to map inputs of single neurons in the mouse primary visual cortex. We read out the expression of viral barcodes in rabies-infected neurons in situ, preserving spatial information. We then matched barcode sequences between starter and presynaptic neurons, mapping the inputs of 385 neurons and identifying 7,814 putative synaptic connections, revealing layer- and cell-type-specific local connectivity rules and topographic organization of long-range inputs. These results show that BRISC can simultaneously resolve the synaptic connectivity of hundreds of neurons, enabling reconstruction of neural circuits at an unprecedented scale.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the need for a more efficient method to map synaptic connectivity in neural circuits, as current techniques are laborious and limited to local connections. The background highlights the importance of understanding how neural networks perform computations through their synaptic architecture.
Design and population
The study developed a novel method called Barcoded Rabies In Situ Connectomics (BRISC) to map both local and long-range monosynaptic inputs of neurons in the mouse primary visual cortex. The method was applied to single neurons in this specific brain region.
Core methods
BRISC utilizes rabies viruses with random molecular barcodes to label neurons, allowing for the simultaneous mapping of synaptic connections. The technique involves reading viral barcode expression in infected neurons and matching sequences between starter and presynaptic neurons to determine connectivity.
Main results
The method successfully mapped the inputs of 385 neurons and identified 7,814 putative synaptic connections, revealing layer- and cell-type-specific local connectivity rules and the topographic organization of long-range inputs.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret these results as demonstrating the capability of BRISC to resolve synaptic connectivity at an unprecedented scale, offering a significant advancement in neural circuit reconstruction.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings have implications for neuroscience research, enabling more comprehensive studies of neural circuits and their functional organization, which could inform future investigations into neural computation and disease mechanisms.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to mouse primary visual cortex and does not address broader applications or other neural regions. The evidence is strong as it directly reports the successful application of BRISC in mapping synaptic connections with high precision.

狂犬病:关于诊断和预防的简要说明

Rabies: a brief note on diagnostics and prophylactics
作者： Farha Deeba, Jaipal Meena, Dinesh Kumar, Harit Kasana, Subhash Chand, Shikha Yadav
摘要中文翻译这篇综述阐明了针对 狂犬病病毒 的抗体定量的新兴方法。生物传感器作为 狂犬病病毒 检测的替代诊断方法:文献综述。回顾 狂犬病 和 疫苗。J 病毒方法 [互联网]。用于检测 狂犬病病毒 特异性抗体的血清学测定的比较综述。狂犬病 的免疫学方面:文献综述。在印度野狐中检测到的 狂犬病病毒 的北极样谱系:案例研究。摘要 狂犬病 仍然是一个巨大的全球公共卫生挑战,归因于狂犬病病毒属病毒。通过引入灭活的 疫苗、狂犬病 免疫球蛋白 (RIG) 和单克隆抗体 (mAb),控制工作取得了显着进展,这些抗体共同提供前和 暴露后预防(PrEP 和 PEP)。然而,在资源匮乏的环境中,PEP 的实施因缺乏高度敏感的诊断检测而受到阻碍。目前,采用世界动物卫生组织(WOAH)和世界卫生组织(WHO)认可的黄金标准技术——快速荧光聚焦抑制试验(RFFIT)和荧光抗体病毒中和(FAVN)测定法——来量化狂犬病病毒中和抗体(VNA)滴度。然而,这些测定是劳动密集型、耗时的,并且需要处理活病毒。值得注意的是,酶联免疫吸附测定 (ELISA) 的最新进展,其特点是快速周转、高特异性和灵敏度、成本效益和可扩展性,显着增强了针对 狂犬病病毒 糖蛋白的抗体的检测。我们还讨论了检测开发过程中遇到的主要技术障碍。展望未来,该...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
这项研究解决了对 狂犬病病毒 改进诊断方法的需求,特别是在资源匮乏的环境中,当前技术因其复杂性和活病毒处理的要求而受到限制。
设计与对象
该研究回顾了 狂犬病病毒 诊断和预防方法,重点关注人类和动物 狂犬病,重点是 疫苗 质量控制和接种个体的抗体检测。
核心方法
该研究讨论了用于定量 狂犬病病毒 糖蛋白抗体的 ELISA 技术的开发和验证,以及用于 疫苗 中 狂犬病病毒 定量的实时 RT-PCR 测定,以及脑脊液中 狂犬病病毒 诊断 宏基因组下一代测序的使用。
主要结果
该研究报告了一种用于测量人类 狂犬病 疫苗 糖蛋白的新型 ELISA 的开发,以及对 狂犬病病毒 定量的实时 RT-PCR 测定的验证。它还强调了使用宏基因组测序进行 狂犬病病毒 检测。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为 狂犬病 诊断的重大进步,为抗体检测和病毒定量提供了更有效和可扩展的方法,这可以改善 疫苗 的质量控制和血清学监测。
科研与公卫意义
这些创新可以提高 狂犬病 诊断的准确性和速度,特别是在资源有限的地区,并支持更好的 疫苗 监测和 狂犬病 控制的公共卫生举措。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于对现有方法的回顾,没有提供原始实验数据,这限制了证据的强度。然而,它提供了当前和新兴诊断技术的全面概述。

Rabies: a brief note on diagnostics and prophylactics

Authors: Farha Deeba, Jaipal Meena, Dinesh Kumar, Harit Kasana, Subhash Chand, Shikha Yadav
Original abstract (English)
This review elucidates emerging methodologies for antibody quantification against rabies virus. Biosensor as an alternative diagnostic method for rabies virus detection: A literature review. Review on Rabies and Vaccines. J Virol Methods [Internet]. A comparative review of serological assays for the detection of rabies virus-specific antibodies. Immunological aspects of rabies: a literature review. Arctic-like lineage of rabies virus detected in Indian wild fox: a case study. Abstract Rabies remains a formidable global public health challenge, attributable to viruses of the genus Lyssavirus. Control efforts have progressed markedly through the introduction of inactivated vaccines, rabies immunoglobulin (RIG), and monoclonal antibodies (mAbs), which collectively provide both pre- and post-exposure prophylaxis (PrEP and PEP). In low-resource environments, however, PEP implementation is hindered by the lack of highly sensitive diagnostic assays. At present, the gold-standard techniques endorsed by the World Organization for Animal Health (WOAH) and the World Health Organization (WHO) — namely the Rapid Fluorescent Focus Inhibition Test (RFFIT) and the Fluorescent Antibody Virus Neutralization (FAVN) assay — are employed to quantify rabies virus neutralizing antibody (VNA) titers. These assays, nonetheless, are labor-intensive, time-consuming, and necessitate handling of live virus. Notably, the recent advancement of an Enzyme-Linked Immunosorbent Assay (ELISA) — characterized by rapid turnaround, high specificity and sensitivity, cost-effectiveness, and scalability has significantly enhanced detection of antibodies directed against the rabies virus glycoprotein. We also discuss the principle technical obstacles encountered during the assay development. Looking forward, th...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study addresses the need for improved diagnostic methods for rabies virus, particularly in low-resource settings where current techniques are limited by their complexity and requirements for live virus handling.
Design and population
The study is a review of methodologies for rabies virus diagnostics and prophylactics, focusing on human and animal rabies, with an emphasis on vaccine quality control and antibody detection in vaccinated individuals.
Core methods
The study discusses the development and validation of ELISA techniques for quantifying rabies virus glycoprotein antibodies, as well as real-time RT-PCR assays for rabies virus quantification in vaccines and the use of metagenomic next-generation sequencing for rabies virus diagnosis in cerebrospinal fluid.
Main results
The study reports the development of a novel ELISA for measuring glycoprotein in human rabies vaccines and the validation of a real-time RT-PCR assay for rabies virus quantification. It also highlights the use of metagenomic sequencing for rabies virus detection.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret these findings as significant advancements in rabies diagnostics, offering more efficient and scalable methods for antibody detection and virus quantification, which could improve vaccine quality control and serological monitoring.
Scientific and public-health significance
These innovations can enhance the accuracy and speed of rabies diagnostics, particularly in resource-limited areas, and support better vaccine monitoring and public health initiatives for rabies control.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on a review of existing methodologies and does not present original experimental data, which limits the strength of the evidence. However, it provides a comprehensive overview of current and emerging diagnostic techniques.
