几内亚2018-2019年犬咬伤病例及狂犬病的流行病学特征

Epidemiological profile of dog bite cases and rabies in Guinea, 2018-2019
作者： Fassou Kourouma, Patrick Mavungu Ngoma, Salomon Corvil, Daouda Bangoura, Briana Lucido, Kristin Baskerville, Lise Diane Martel
摘要中文翻译简介:在几内亚,2011年至2017年期间,卫生机构记录了95例临床犬狂犬病病例,10,153例咬伤病例,兽医站记录了50例临床人类狂犬病病例。该疾病的概况尚不清楚。本研究描述了 狂犬病 的流行病学概况,以指导卫生部和畜牧业部的决策。方法:使用卫生部和畜牧部 2018 年 1 月至 2019 年 8 月的咬合和 狂犬病 数据库进行了描述性 发病率 研究。我们计算了每 100,000 人的中位年龄、频率和区域咬合 发病率。结果:总体而言,记录了 1,953 起咬伤病例,发病率 为每 100,000 人中有 10 起,中位年龄为 14 岁。在被咬伤者中,75% (1458/1953) 受益于暴露后 疫苗接种 (PEV);10 名狂犬病人类中无人寻求 疫苗接种,全部死亡。在随访的1662只咬人狗中,观察到937只(56%),其中159只(17%)出现狂犬病症状并被安乐死,并采样了27只(17%),其中20只(74%)通过PCR检测确诊为狂犬病。N’Zérékoré 和 Fria 的 发病率 每 100,000 名居民被咬伤超过 50 次;40% 的犬类 狂犬病 病例来自法拉纳。在 发病率 和确诊 狂犬病 病例百分比较高的地区,有 15,000 只狗接种了疫苗。结论:15岁以下人群受影响最大。我们建议在发病率低的地区改善狗咬伤和狂犬病 监测的情况,建立实验室采样能力,检测疑似犬类病例,并对狗群进行详尽的普查。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
在几内亚,2011年至2017年期间,卫生机构记录了95例临床犬狂犬病病例,10,153例咬伤病例,兽医站记录了50例临床人类狂犬病病例。该疾病的概况尚不清楚。
设计与对象
使用卫生部和畜牧业部 2018 年 1 月至 2019 年 8 月的咬合和 狂犬病 数据库进行了一项描述性 发病率 研究。总体而言,记录了 1,953 起咬伤病例,发病率 为每 100,000 人中有 10 起,中位年龄为 14 岁。
核心方法
我们计算了每 100,000 人的中位年龄、频率和区域咬合 发病率。在随访的1662只咬人狗中,观察到937只(56%),其中159只(17%)出现狂犬病症状并被安乐死,并采样了27只(17%),其中20只(74%)通过PCR检测确诊为狂犬病。
主要结果
在被咬伤者中,75% (1458/1953) 受益于暴露后 疫苗接种 (PEV);10 名狂犬病人类中无人寻求 疫苗接种,全部死亡。N’Zérékoré 和 Fria 的 发病率 每 100,000 名居民被咬伤超过 50 次;40% 的犬类 狂犬病 病例来自法拉纳。
解释与创新
15 岁以下的人受影响最大。本研究描述了 狂犬病 的流行病学概况,以指导卫生部和畜牧业部的决策。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为 发病率 高发地区和确诊 狂犬病 病例的地区制定有针对性的 发病率 策略提供参考。我们建议在发病率低的地区改善狗咬伤和狂犬病 监测的情况,建立实验室采样能力,检测疑似犬类病例,并对狗群进行详尽的普查。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于数据库,不包括详细的实验室或统计程序。证据是中等的,因为它依赖于描述性数据而没有进一步分析。

Epidemiological profile of dog bite cases and rabies in Guinea, 2018-2019

Authors: Fassou Kourouma, Patrick Mavungu Ngoma, Salomon Corvil, Daouda Bangoura, Briana Lucido, Kristin Baskerville, Lise Diane Martel
Original abstract (English)
Introduction: In Guinea, between 2011 and 2017, there were 95 clinical canine rabies cases, 10,153 cases of bites recorded in the health facilities, and 50 clinical human rabies cases recorded in the veterinary post. The profile of the disease is not well known. This study describes the epidemiological profile of rabies to guide the decision-making of the Ministries of Health and Livestock. Methods: A descriptive incidence study was carried out using January 2018–August 2019 bite and rabies databases of the Ministries of Health and Livestock. We calculated median age, frequency, and district bite incidence per 100,000 population. Results: Overall, 1,953 bite cases were recorded, with an incidence of 10 per 100,000 population and a median age of 14 years. Of those bitten, 75% (1458/1953) benefited from post-exposure vaccination (PEV); none of the 10 rabid humans sought vaccination, and all died. Of the 1,662 biting dogs followed up, 937 (56%) were observed, of which 159 (17%) developed rabies symptoms and were euthanised, and 27 (17%) were sampled, 20 (74%) of which were confirmed for rabies by PCR test. N’Zérékoré and Fria had an incidence of >50 bites per 100,000 inhabitants; 40% of canine rabies cases were from Faranah. Fifteen thousand dogs were vaccinated in districts with high incidence and percentage of confirmed rabies cases. Conclusion: People under 15 years old were most affected. We suggest improving dog bite and rabies surveillance in the district with low incidence, building laboratory sampling capacity, testing suspected canine cases, and conducting an exhaustive census among the dog population.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
In Guinea, between 2011 and 2017, there were 95 clinical canine rabies cases, 10,153 cases of bites recorded in the health facilities, and 50 clinical human rabies cases recorded in the veterinary post. The profile of the disease is not well known.
Design and population
A descriptive incidence study was carried out using January 2018–August 2019 bite and rabies databases of the Ministries of Health and Livestock. Overall, 1,953 bite cases were recorded, with an incidence of 10 per 100,000 population and a median age of 14 years.
Core methods
We calculated median age, frequency, and district bite incidence per 100,000 population. Of the 1,662 biting dogs followed up, 937 (56%) were observed, of which 159 (17%) developed rabies symptoms and were euthanised, and 27 (17%) were sampled, 20 (74%) of which were confirmed for rabies by PCR test.
Main results
Of those bitten, 75% (1458/1953) benefited from post-exposure vaccination (PEV); none of the 10 rabid humans sought vaccination, and all died. N’Zérékoré and Fria had an incidence of >50 bites per 100,000 inhabitants; 40% of canine rabies cases were from Faranah.
Interpretation and novelty
People under 15 years old were most affected. This study describes the epidemiological profile of rabies to guide the decision-making of the Ministries of Health and Livestock.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform targeted vaccination strategies in districts with high incidence and confirmed rabies cases. We suggest improving dog bite and rabies surveillance in the district with low incidence, building laboratory sampling capacity, testing suspected canine cases, and conducting an exhaustive census among the dog population.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on databases and does not include detailed laboratory or statistical procedures. The evidence is moderate as it relies on descriptive data without further analysis.

PERCEPÇÓO SOBRE RAIVA E INTERAÇÓ ENTRE OS HABITANTES DO MUNICIPIO DE CHÍ GRANDE - PE 对 狂犬病 在 CHÍ GRANDE 市居民中的认知 - PE PERCEPCÓN SOBRE LA RABIA ENTRE LOS HABITANTES DEL MUNICIPIO DE CHË GRANDE - 聚乙烯

PERCEPÇÃO SOBRE RAIVA E INTERAÇÃO ENTRE OS HABITANTES DO MUNICÍPIO DE CHÃ GRANDE - PE PERCEPTION OF RABIES AMONG THE INHABITANTS OF CHÃ GRANDE MUNICIPALITY - PE PERCEPCIÓN SOBRE LA RABIA ENTRE LOS HABITANTES DEL MUNICIPIO DE CHÃ GRANDE - PE
作者： Joelma Silvestre dos Santos Silva, Vitor Gabriel Pedro da Silva, Débora Taisa da Silva, Georgia Cybelle dos Santos Silva Barros, Albérico Queiroz Salgueiro de Souza, Luiz Augustinho Menezes da Silva
摘要中文翻译狂犬病是一种高致死率的人兽共患病,其在野生动物循环中的持续存在对公共卫生构成挑战,尤其是在人类、家畜和野生动物互动频繁的农村地区。本研究旨在分析巴西佩恩塔布斯州查格兰德地区农村居民对狂犬病的认知,描述其与野生动物的互动情况及其潜在的流行病学风险。研究通过与农村居民的访谈进行,调查他们对狂犬病的了解、与家畜和野生动物接触的相关行为以及涉及的物种。数据根据所提及物种的流行病学重要性进行分析。结果显示,大多数受访者认识到狂犬病是一种严重且致命的疾病。报告了频繁与野生动物的互动,包括狩猎和反复的目击,其中刺猬和狐狸尤为突出,后者是狂犬病陆地野生动物循环的一部分。还提到了一些无直接流行病学意义的物种,显示出农村居民对野生动物相关卫生风险的认知存在不足。结论认为,这些互动可能构成维持狂犬病循环的潜在风险,强调了开展健康教育、综合监测和单一卫生体系策略的必要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
狂犬病是一种高致死率的人兽共患病,其在野生动物循环中的持续存在对公共卫生构成挑战,尤其是在人类、家畜和野生动物互动频繁的农村地区。
设计与对象
我们对伯南布哥州大查农村地区的人口进行了客观分析,并以与母亲和流行病学的潜力为特征。
核心方法
为实现农村地区的农村发展、调查或相关的实践、与国内动物和森林相关的实践关系以及涉及互动的特别事项而进行的调查。
主要结果
我们的结果是,大多数人的行动都导致了严重和致命的后果。
解释与创新
经常与妈妈们进行互动的论坛,包括观看和观看记录、与他人交谈以及分享,这是与西克洛·西尔维斯特雷瓦的最后一个整合。
科研与公卫意义
还提到了一些无直接流行病学意义的物种,显示出农村居民对野生动物相关卫生风险的认知存在不足。
局限与证据强度
结论认为,这些互动可能构成维持狂犬病循环的潜在风险,强调了开展健康教育、综合监测和单一卫生体系策略的必要性。

PERCEPÇÃO SOBRE RAIVA E INTERAÇÃO ENTRE OS HABITANTES DO MUNICÍPIO DE CHÃ GRANDE - PE PERCEPTION OF RABIES AMONG THE INHABITANTS OF CHÃ GRANDE MUNICIPALITY - PE PERCEPCIÓN SOBRE LA RABIA ENTRE LOS HABITANTES DEL MUNICIPIO DE CHÃ GRANDE - PE

Authors: Joelma Silvestre dos Santos Silva, Vitor Gabriel Pedro da Silva, Débora Taisa da Silva, Georgia Cybelle dos Santos Silva Barros, Albérico Queiroz Salgueiro de Souza, Luiz Augustinho Menezes da Silva
Original abstract (English)
A raiva é uma zoonose viral de elevada letalidade, cuja persistência no ciclo silvestre representa um desafio para a saúde pública, especialmente em áreas rurais com intensa interação entre humanos, animais domésticos e fauna silvestre. Este estudo teve como objetivo analisar a percepção da população rural de Chã Grande, Pernambuco, sobre a raiva, caracterizar as interações com mamíferos silvestres e seus potenciais riscos epidemiológicos. A pesquisa foi realizada por entrevistas com moradores da zona rural, investigando o conhecimento sobre a doença, práticas relacionadas ao contato com animais domésticos e silvestres e as espécies envolvidas nessas interações. Os dados foram analisados considerando a relevância epidemiológica das espécies citadas. Os resultados indicaram que a maioria dos entrevistados reconhece a raiva como doença grave e fatal. Foram relatadas interações frequentes com mamíferos silvestres, incluindo caça e avistamentos recorrentes, com destaque para o tatu e a raposa, esta última integrante do ciclo silvestre terrestre da raiva. Também foram mencionadas espécies sem relevância epidemiológica direta, evidenciando lacunas na percepção do risco sanitário associado à fauna silvestre. Conclui-se que essas interações configuram risco potencial para a manutenção do ciclo da raiva, reforçando a necessidade de ações educativas, vigilância integrada e estratégias no âmbito da Saúde Única.

Question and background
A raiva é uma zoonose viral de elevada letalidade, cuja persistência no ciclo silvestre representa um desafio para a saúde pública, especialmente em áreas rurais com intensa interação entre humanos, animais domésticos e fauna silvestre.

从一刀切到因地制宜:10 天观察规则——需要改变

From one-size-fits-all to context-specific: the 10-day observation rule-a need for change
作者： Xin Wang
摘要中文翻译世卫组织的“零到 30”战略计划依赖于为资源丰富的环境设计的 暴露后预防 指南。本政策简报认为,高负担国家10天观察规则的执行差距源于根本不可行,暴露出世卫组织指南制定中的两个范式缺陷:一刀切的思维和对执行科学重视不够。当公众将 10 天规则误解为“观望”而不是“在观察动物的同时立即启动 PEP”时,三个生物学事实使这种误解变得致命:有症状的 狂犬病 几乎 100% 致命且无法治疗;单次疫苗剂量不能立即诱导足够的中和抗体;颅面 III 类咬伤的潜伏期可短至 5-10 天,比完整观察窗口还短。在此窗口期间等待动物观察而不启动 PEP 意味着错过有效预防的唯一机会。来自多个国家的证据表明,在 95% 的 狂犬病 死亡发生的环境中,该规则的先决条件几乎从未得到满足。这种误解导致了可预防的死亡,并引发了与指南相关的 狂犬病 恐惧症 (GARP)。我们提出五项修订来完善(而不是放弃)该规则。重塑范式是实现“30 年前零排放”的道德先决条件。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了修改世卫组织 暴露后预防 指南的需要,该指南是为资源丰富的环境设计的,但在高负担国家可能不可行。作者强调了对 10 天观察规则的误解,认为这是一种“观望”方法,而不是一种及时干预策略。
设计与对象
该研究是一份政策简报,分析了高负担国家 10 天观察规则的执行差距,95% 的 狂犬病 死亡都发生在这些国家。它重点关注对规则的误解及其对 狂犬病 预防的后果。
核心方法
这些方法涉及对来自多个国家的证据进行审查,表明该规则的先决条件很少得到满足。作者还研究了导致对该规则的误解的危险的生物学事实,例如有症状的 狂犬病 的死亡率接近 100%,以及单次 疫苗 剂量的局限性。
主要结果
来自多个国家的证据表明,在 95% 的 狂犬病 死亡发生的环境中,该规则的先决条件几乎从未得到满足。在此窗口期间等待动物观察而不启动 PEP 意味着错过有效预防的唯一机会。
解释与创新
作者解释说,对 10 天规则的误解导致了可预防的死亡和 狂犬病 恐惧症。他们提议对规则进行五项修订以解决这些问题,强调需要针对具体情况制定指导方针。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明需要制定针对具体情况的 暴露后预防 预防策略,特别是在高负担国家,以确保有效的 暴露后预防 并减少可预防的死亡。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅基于摘要证据,不提供详细的统计或方法分析。证据强度中等,因为它依赖于政策和观察数据而不是实验结果。

From one-size-fits-all to context-specific: the 10-day observation rule-a need for change

Authors: Xin Wang
Original abstract (English)
WHO’s ‘Zero by 30’ strategic plan relies on post-exposure prophylaxis guidelines designed for high-resource settings. This policy brief argues that the implementation gap of the 10-day observation rule in high-burden countries stems from fundamental infeasibility, exposing two paradigm defects in WHO guideline development: one-size-fits-all thinking and insufficient attention to implementation science. When the public misinterprets the 10-day rule as “wait and see” rather than “initiate PEP immediately while observing the animal,” three biological facts render this misinterpretation fatal: symptomatic rabies is nearly 100% fatal and untreatable; a single vaccine dose cannot promptly induce sufficient neutralizing antibodies; and the incubation period for craniofacial Category III bites can be as short as 5–10 days—shorter than the full observation window. Waiting for animal observation without initiating PEP during this window means missing the only opportunity for effective prevention. Evidence from multiple countries shows that the rule’s preconditions are almost never met in settings where 95% of rabies deaths occur. This misinterpretation has led to preventable deaths and given rise to guideline-associated rabies phobia (GARP). We propose five revisions to refine—not discard—the rule. Reshaping the paradigm is an ethical prerequisite for achieving ‘Zero by 30’.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the need to revise WHO’s post-exposure prophylaxis guidelines for rabies, which are designed for high-resource settings but may not be feasible in high-burden countries. The authors highlight the misinterpretation of the 10-day observation rule as a ‘wait and see’ approach rather than a prompt intervention strategy.
Design and population
The study is a policy brief analyzing the implementation gap of the 10-day observation rule in high-burden countries, where 95% of rabies deaths occur. It focuses on the misinterpretation of the rule and its consequences for rabies prevention.
Core methods
The methods involve a review of evidence from multiple countries showing the rule’s preconditions are rarely met. The authors also examine the biological facts that make the misinterpretation of the rule dangerous, such as the near 100% fatality rate of symptomatic rabies and the limitations of a single vaccine dose.
Main results
Evidence from multiple countries shows that the rule’s preconditions are almost never met in settings where 95% of rabies deaths occur. Waiting for animal observation without initiating PEP during this window means missing the only opportunity for effective prevention.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that the misinterpretation of the 10-day rule has led to preventable deaths and rabies phobia. They propose five revisions to the rule to address these issues, emphasizing the need for context-specific guidelines.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the need for context-specific rabies prevention strategies, particularly in high-burden countries, to ensure effective post-exposure prophylaxis and reduce preventable deaths.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on abstract-only evidence and does not provide detailed statistical or methodological analysis. The evidence strength is moderate, as it relies on policy and observational data rather than experimental results.

研究小鼠声音交流中初级运动皮层的参与

Investigating Primary Motor Cortex Involvement in Mouse Vocal Communication
作者： Elena N. Duryea Waidmann
摘要中文翻译语音通信用于向其他人传输一系列信息。虽然许多发声是与生俱来的,但有些物种可以学习改变它们的声音输出。这被称为声音学习,它是一种罕见的特征,也是人类口语和鸣禽歌曲的必要组成部分。对人类和鸣禽的比较研究表明,趋同的遗传和神经专业化可能会驱动这些习得的发声行为。这些高级发声学习者的两个关键特征是:i)前脑中专门的初级运动区域,其活动与发声相关;ii)从初级运动皮层到脑干发声运动神经元的密集单突触投射。我们实验室的工作表明,小鼠超声波发声 (USV) 系统具有一些与高级发声学习者相似的功能。USV 的产生是与生俱来的,但小鼠的音节库表现出上下文相关的变化。小鼠还可能拥有初级的喉部运动皮层(LMC),对疑核(Amb)中的喉部运动神经元有稀疏的直接投射,并且稀疏的群体对声带肌肉组织具有短潜伏期的功能控制。在这些研究之前,人们一直认为小鼠在超声波发声的产生中缺乏任何皮质参与。小鼠鸣叫的有限行为灵活性和声带肌肉前脑表征的证据表明,声乐学习可能存在于一个连续体中,小鼠处于连续体的有限末端,能够对 USV 的产生施加一些皮质控制。这一假设认为,声音学习不是一种全有或全无的能力,而是存在于一系列连续的声音行为中,并得到相应范围的神经和遗传专业化的支持。在这篇论文中,我建议通过研究初级运动皮层在小鼠发声行为中是否发挥作用来检验连续统假设。我首先描述了稀疏直接投射在声带肌肉控制中的作用。我使用复制缺陷 狂犬病 追踪来确认从 LMC 的第 5 层神经元到脑干喉部运动神经元存在稀疏投影,并且还发现前 M1 区域口面运动皮层 (OMC) 也具有直接的皮质运动神经元投影。然后,我使用 PLXNA1fl/fl;RBP4-CRE 转基因小鼠(从皮质到 Amb 的神经支配更密集)来测试该回路增强的功能后果。我使用配对的皮质内微刺激(ICMS)和肌电图(EMG),发现这些小鼠的喉部对皮质刺激的反应更快。这些发现表明,直接皮质运动神经元投射的增强可以产生更快的皮质对发声肌的控制。然后我测试了小鼠 M1 是否具有与发声行为相关的神经活动。我将 LMC 和 OMC 中的电生理记录与自由行为和发声的男性的视频和音频监控结合起来。我发现了稀疏的神经元群,其活动随着 USV 的产生而受到调节。这是小鼠 M1 发声相关活动的第一份报告。最后,我开发了一种新方法来识别发声小鼠中的单个 USV 源。该方法使用可穿戴微型麦克风来检测 USV,并根据简单的幅度差异将其分配给单个动物。我可靠地确定了负责发声的个体,并发现雌性小鼠在雄性小鼠和雌性小鼠中产生的 USV 比之前假设的要少得多。该方法可以进一步研究社交 USV 生产过程中个体间的声音动态和行为。总的来说,我的发现支持声音学习的连续统一体假设。从 M1 到喉部运动神经元的直接皮质输入似乎是一个进化节点,可以更快地控制发声肌肉组织。这些相同的 M1 区域具有有限但存在与发声相关活动的种群。这表明声带肌肉的稀疏皮质表征可能得以保留。这些结果让我们更深入地了解哺乳动物大脑中发声的皮层控制以及更高级的发声学习的 进化。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查初级运动皮层是否在小鼠发声行为中发挥作用,该研究基于这样的假设:发声学习存在于连续体中,且小鼠处于有限端。
设计与对象
该研究使用脑干发声运动神经元皮质神经支配增强的转基因小鼠以及用于发声监测的自由行为雄性小鼠进行。
核心方法
这些方法包括用于绘制皮质投影的复制缺陷 狂犬病 追踪、用于评估功能后果的配对皮质内微刺激 (ICMS) 和肌电图 (EMG),以及用于识别个体超声发声 (USV) 源的可穿戴微型麦克风。
主要结果
研究发现,从喉部运动皮层(LMC)第5层神经元到脑干喉部运动神经元存在稀疏的直接投射,而前M1区域口面运动皮层(OMC)也有直接的皮质运动神经元投射。此外,M1 中稀疏的神经元群在 USV 产生过程中表现出活动调节。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,从 M1 到喉部运动神经元的直接皮质输入可能代表了更快控制发声肌肉组织的进化节点,并且 M1 区域的数量有限,但存在与发声相关的活动,支持了发声学习的连续统一体假设。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果为哺乳动物大脑中发声的皮层控制和更高级的发声学习的 进化 提供了更深入的了解,这可能为未来发声行为及其神经基础的研究提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对单一物种的关注和转基因模型的使用而受到限制,但研究结果得到了直接实验证据的支持,并提供了一种识别个体 USV 来源的新方法。

Investigating Primary Motor Cortex Involvement in Mouse Vocal Communication

Authors: Elena N. Duryea Waidmann
Original abstract (English)
Vocal communication is used to transmit a range of information to other individuals. While many vocalizations are innate, some species can learn to change their vocal output. This is termed vocal-learning, and it is a rare trait and a necessary building block for human spoken language and songbird song. Comparative work in humans and songbirds suggests that convergent genetic and neural specializations may drive these learned vocal behaviors. Two key features of these advanced vocal learners are: i) specialized primary motor regions in the forebrain with activity tied to vocalization; and ii) dense monosynaptic projections from the primary motor cortex to brainstem vocal motor neurons. Work from our lab suggests that the mouse ultrasonic vocalization (USV) system has some features similar to those found in advanced vocal learners. USV production is innate, but mice show context-dependent changes in their syllable repertoire. Mice may also possess a rudimentary laryngeal motor cortex (LMC), with a sparse direct projection to laryngeal motor neurons in the nucleus ambiguus (Amb) and sparse populations with short-latency functional control over vocal musculature. Prior to these studies, mice have been assumed to lack any cortical involvement in the production of ultrasonic vocalizations. The limited behavioral flexibility of mouse song and evidence of forebrain representations of vocal muscles suggest that vocal learning may exist over a continuum, with mice being at the limited end of the continuum, able to exert some cortical control over USV production. This hypothesis argues that vocal learning is not an all-or-nothing ability, but exists on a continuous range of vocal behavior, supported by a corresponding range of neural and genetic specializations. In this thesis, I proposed to test the continuum hypothesis, by investigating whether there is a role of primary motor cortex in mouse vocal behavior. I first characterized the role of the sparse direct projection in the control of vocal muscles. I used replication-deficient rabies tracing to confirm that there is a sparse projection from layer 5 neurons of LMC to brainstem laryngeal motor neurons, and also found that an anterior M1 region, orofacial motor cortex (OMC), also has a direct cortico-motoneuronal projection. I then used PLXNA1fl/fl;RBP4-CRE transgenic mice, which have denser innervation from the cortex to Amb, to test the functional consequences of enhancement of this circuit. I used paired intracortical microstimulation (ICMS) and electromyography (EMG) and found faster responses in the larynx of these mice to cortical stimulations. These findings show that the enhancement of direct cortico-motoneuronal projections produce faster cortical control over vocal muscles. I then tested whether mouse M1 has any neural activity associated with vocalization behavior. I combined electrophysiological recordings in LMC and OMC with video and audio monitoring of freely behaving and vocalizing males. I identified sparse populations of neurons with activity modulated over the production of bouts of USVs. This is the first report of vocalization-related activity in mouse M1. Finally, I developed a novel method to identify individual USV sources in pairs of vocalizing mice. This method used wearable miniature microphones to detect and assign USVs to a single animal based on simple amplitude differences. I reliably identified the individual responsible for the vocalization, and found that female mice produce far fewer USVs in a male- female pair than previously assumed. This method allows further study of the inter-individual vocal dynamics and behavior during the production of social USV. Overall, my findings support the continuum hypothesis of vocal learning. The direct cortical input from M1 to laryngeal motor neurons appears to be an evolutionary node to have faster control of vocal musculature. These same M1 regions have limited but present populations with vocalization-associated activity. This suggests that a sparse cortical representation for vocal muscles may be conserved. These results provide greater insight into the cortical control of vocalization in the mammalian brain and the evolution of more advanced vocal learning.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate whether the primary motor cortex plays a role in mouse vocal behavior, building on the hypothesis that vocal learning exists on a continuum with mice at the limited end.
Design and population
The study was conducted using transgenic mice with enhanced cortical innervation to the brainstem vocal motor neurons, as well as freely behaving male mice for vocalization monitoring.
Core methods
The methods included replication-deficient rabies tracing to map cortical projections, paired intracortical microstimulation (ICMS) and electromyography (EMG) to assess functional consequences, and wearable miniature microphones to identify individual ultrasonic vocalization (USV) sources.
Main results
The study found that there is a sparse direct projection from the layer 5 neurons of the laryngeal motor cortex (LMC) to brainstem laryngeal motor neurons, and an anterior M1 region, orofacial motor cortex (OMC), also has a direct cortico-motoneuronal projection. Additionally, sparse populations of neurons in M1 showed activity modulated during USV production.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that the direct cortical input from M1 to laryngeal motor neurons may represent an evolutionary node for faster control of vocal musculature, and that M1 regions have limited but present populations with vocalization-associated activity, supporting the continuum hypothesis of vocal learning.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results provide greater insight into the cortical control of vocalization in the mammalian brain and the evolution of more advanced vocal learning, which may inform future studies on vocal behavior and its neural basis.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its focus on a single species and the use of a transgenic model, but the findings are supported by direct experimental evidence and provide a novel method for identifying individual USV sources.

南美洲和中美洲野生猫科动物袭击人类:2014-2025 年法属圭亚那案例系列和文献综述。

Wild Felid Attacks on Humans in South and Central America: A 2014-2025 Case Series in French Guiana and Literature Review.
作者： Elias Zaoui, Corentin Knoploch, Anissa Desmoulin, Rémi Mutricy, Alexis Fremery, Brigitte Roman-Laverdure, Stéphanie Barthe, Loïc Epelboin, Alexis Frémery
摘要中文翻译野生猫科动物是顶级掠食者,在热带生态系统中发挥着至关重要的作用。在南美洲,人类与大型猫科动物——尤其是美洲虎(Panthera onca)和美洲狮(Puma concolor)——之间的相互作用的报道越来越多,引起了公众的关注。然而,对人类的攻击的记录仍然很少,而且它们的频率可能被高估了。这项研究通过病例系列和文献综述评估了法属圭亚那和南美洲野生猫科动物袭击的规模。我们对 2014 年 1 月至 2025 年 12 月期间法属圭亚那医疗机构管理的所有野生猫科动物袭击事件进行了回顾性观察研究。病例是通过医院急诊科、区域 狂犬病 治疗 中心和远程诊所发现的。审查了医疗记录的社会人口统计数据、情况、临床结果和管理。系统文献综述(即 PubMed、ScienceDirect、SciELO 和 LILACS)确定了 1990 年至 2025 年期间涉及南美野生猫科动物的攻击记录。涉及圈养动物或缺乏方法细节的报告被排除在外。法属圭亚那发生了四起袭击事件,没有造成人员死亡,但有 1 起袭击事件造成严重后遗症。文献综述确定了 11 个事件,主要归因于美洲虎,偶尔也归因于美洲狮。袭击事件很少见,通常与环境因素有关,例如邻近森林、家畜或动物状况受损。据报道,伤势严重,其中 3 人死亡。在南美洲,野生猫科动物对人类的攻击仍然极为罕见,通常与可识别的环境或人为因素有关,而不是与固有的攻击性有关。栖息地保护和减少人类与野生动物的重叠是关键的预防策略。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
野生猫科动物是顶级掠食者,在热带生态系统中发挥着至关重要的作用。在南美洲,人类与大型猫科动物——尤其是美洲虎(Panthera onca)和美洲狮(Puma concolor)——之间的相互作用的报道越来越多,引起了公众的关注。在南美洲,野生猫科动物对人类的攻击仍然极为罕见,通常与可识别的环境或人为因素有关,而不是与固有的攻击性有关。
证据基础与方法
我们对 2014 年 1 月至 2025 年 12 月期间法属圭亚那医疗机构管理的所有野生猫科动物袭击事件进行了回顾性观察研究。病例是通过医院急诊科、区域 狂犬病 治疗 中心和远程诊所发现的。系统文献综述(即 PubMed、ScienceDirect、SciELO 和 LILACS)确定了 1990 年至 2025 年期间涉及南美野生猫科动物的攻击记录。涉及圈养动物或缺乏方法细节的报告被排除在外。
共识与结论
法属圭亚那发生了四起袭击事件,没有造成人员死亡,但有 1 起袭击事件造成严重后遗症。文献综述确定了 11 个事件,主要归因于美洲虎,偶尔也归因于美洲狮。据报道,伤势严重,其中 3 人死亡。袭击事件很少见,通常与环境因素有关,例如邻近森林、家畜或动物状况受损。
争议与缺口
然而,对人类的攻击的记录仍然很少,而且它们的频率可能被高估了。所提供的证据没有报告评估法属圭亚那和南美洲野生猫科动物袭击规模的具体方法。
科研与实践启示
栖息地保护和减少人类与野生动物的重叠是关键的预防策略。所提供的证据并未报告对 狂犬病病毒 预防或管理的具体研究或实践影响。

Wild Felid Attacks on Humans in South and Central America: A 2014-2025 Case Series in French Guiana and Literature Review.

Authors: Elias Zaoui, Corentin Knoploch, Anissa Desmoulin, Rémi Mutricy, Alexis Fremery, Brigitte Roman-Laverdure, Stéphanie Barthe, Loïc Epelboin, Alexis Frémery
Original abstract (English)
Wild felids are apex predators that play a crucial role in tropical ecosystems. In South America, interactions between humans and large felids-particularly jaguars ( Panthera onca ) and pumas ( Puma concolor )-are increasingly reported, raising public concern. However, attacks on humans remain poorly documented, and their frequency is likely overestimated. This study assessed the scale of wild felid attacks in French Guiana and South America through a case series and literature review. We conducted a retrospective observational study of all wild felid attacks managed in healthcare facilities in French Guiana between January 2014 and December 2025. Cases were identified through hospital emergency departments, the regional rabies treatment center, and remote clinics. Medical records were reviewed for sociodemographic data, circumstances, clinical outcomes, and management. A systematic literature review (ie, PubMed, ScienceDirect, SciELO, and LILACS) identified documented attacks involving South American wild felids from 1990 to 2025. Reports involving captive animals or lacking methodologic detail were excluded. Four attacks were recorded in French Guiana, with no fatalities, although 1 case led to significant sequelae. The literature review identified 11 events, mainly attributed to jaguars and occasionally pumas. Attacks were rare and often linked to contextual factors such as forest proximity, domestic animals, or impaired animal condition. Severe injuries were reported, including 3 fatal cases. Wild felid attacks on humans remain exceptionally rare in South America and are usually associated with identifiable environmental or anthropogenic factors rather than inherent aggressiveness. Habitat preservation and reduced human-wildlife overlap are key preventive strategies.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Wild felids are apex predators that play a crucial role in tropical ecosystems. In South America, interactions between humans and large felids-particularly jaguars ( Panthera onca ) and pumas ( Puma concolor )-are increasingly reported, raising public concern. Wild felid attacks on humans remain exceptionally rare in South America and are usually associated with identifiable environmental or anthropogenic factors rather than inherent aggressiveness.
Evidence base and method
We conducted a retrospective observational study of all wild felid attacks managed in healthcare facilities in French Guiana between January 2014 and December 2025. Cases were identified through hospital emergency departments, the regional rabies treatment center, and remote clinics. A systematic literature review (ie, PubMed, ScienceDirect, SciELO, and LILACS) identified documented attacks involving South American wild felids from 1990 to 2025. Reports involving captive animals or lacking methodologic detail were excluded.
Consensus and conclusions
Four attacks were recorded in French Guiana, with no fatalities, although 1 case led to significant sequelae. The literature review identified 11 events, mainly attributed to jaguars and occasionally pumas. Severe injuries were reported, including 3 fatal cases. Attacks were rare and often linked to contextual factors such as forest proximity, domestic animals, or impaired animal condition.
Controversies and gaps
However, attacks on humans remain poorly documented, and their frequency is likely overestimated. The supplied evidence does not report specific methods for assessing the scale of wild felid attacks in French Guiana and South America.
Research and practice implications
Habitat preservation and reduced human-wildlife overlap are key preventive strategies. The supplied evidence does not report specific research or practice implications for rabies virus prevention or management.

区分儿童中犬咬伤与狂犬病漏洞以加强全球消除努力

Distinguishing dog-bite and rabies vulnerability in children to strengthen global elimination efforts
作者： Harish Kumar Tiwari
摘要中文翻译狗介导的 狂犬病 仍然是儿童 死亡率 的一个可预防的原因,特别是在自由漫游狗很常见的低收入和中等收入国家。虽然世界卫生组织主导的“零到30”战略强调大众狗疫苗接种和获得暴露后预防 (PEP),但对影响儿童脆弱性的行为和沟通途径却较少关注。该观点提出了狗咬伤脆弱性和暴露后 狂犬病 脆弱性之间的概念区别。将这些风险混为一谈可能会导致信息分散和错失预防机会。该观点主张采取差异化的面向儿童和面向成人的干预措施,将学校定位为适应性强的平台,将安全行为、信息披露和及时寻求护理纳入现有系统。更明确地协调行为、教育和生物医学组成部分可能会加强全球 狂犬病 消除战略的实施并减少儿科死亡。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
狗介导的 狂犬病 仍然是儿童 死亡率 的一个可预防的原因,特别是在自由漫游狗很常见的低收入和中等收入国家。
证据基础与方法
该综述没有明确报告数据库、检索日期、资格标准、选择方法、纳入研究计数或荟萃分析过程。
共识与结论
该观点提出了狗咬伤脆弱性和暴露后 狂犬病 脆弱性之间的概念区别。更明确地协调行为、教育和生物医学组成部分可能会加强全球 狂犬病 消除战略的实施并减少儿科死亡。
争议与缺口
将这些风险混为一谈可能会导致信息分散和错失预防机会。所提供的证据并未报告与 狂犬病病毒 相关的具体证据差距或未解决的机制。
科研与实践启示
该观点主张采取差异化的面向儿童和面向成人的干预措施,将学校定位为适应性强的平台,将安全行为、信息披露和及时寻求护理纳入现有系统。

Distinguishing dog-bite and rabies vulnerability in children to strengthen global elimination efforts

Authors: Harish Kumar Tiwari
Original abstract (English)
Dog-mediated rabies remains a preventable cause of mortality in children, particularly in low- and middle-income countries where free-roaming dogs are common. While the WHO-led “Zero by 30” strategy emphasises mass dog vaccination and access to post-exposure prophylaxis (PEP), less attention has been paid to the behavioural and communication pathways that shape children’s vulnerability. This Viewpoint proposes a conceptual distinction between vulnerability to dog bites and vulnerability to rabies following exposure. Conflating these risks may contribute to diffuse messaging and missed prevention opportunities. This Viewpoint argues for a differentiated child-facing and adult-facing interventions, with schools positioned as adaptable platforms for integrating safe behaviour, disclosure, and timely care-seeking within existing systems. Aligning behavioural, educational, and biomedical components more explicitly may strengthen implementation of global rabies elimination strategies and reduce paediatric deaths.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Dog-mediated rabies remains a preventable cause of mortality in children, particularly in low- and middle-income countries where free-roaming dogs are common.
Evidence base and method
The review does not explicitly report on the databases, search dates, eligibility criteria, selection method, included-study count, or meta-analysis process.
Consensus and conclusions
This Viewpoint proposes a conceptual distinction between vulnerability to dog bites and vulnerability to rabies following exposure. Aligning behavioural, educational, and biomedical components more explicitly may strengthen implementation of global rabies elimination strategies and reduce paediatric deaths.
Controversies and gaps
Conflating these risks may contribute to diffuse messaging and missed prevention opportunities. The supplied evidence does not report on specific evidence gaps or unresolved mechanisms related to rabies virus.
Research and practice implications
This Viewpoint argues for a differentiated child-facing and adult-facing interventions, with schools positioned as adaptable platforms for integrating safe behaviour, disclosure, and timely care-seeking within existing systems.

症状性狂犬病极高致死率的潜在生物学屏障:神经侵袭、免疫逃逸和血脑屏障限制

Biological barriers underlying the extremely high fatality of symptomatic rabies: neuroinvasion, immune evasion, and blood–brain barrier restriction
作者： Chen Sun, Xiao Guo, Liang Xiao, Wenxin Wang, Ge Shi, Chenci Liu, Shunyao Chen, Zhao-Hui Tang
摘要中文翻译狂犬病 仍然是最可预防的致命感染之一,但一旦出现临床症状,存活率极其罕见。这一悖论不能简单地用缺乏单一有效的 抗病毒药物 药物来解释。在这篇综述中,我们将症状性 狂犬病 定义为一个连续的屏障驱动的疾病过程,其中神经侵袭、免疫逃避和血脑屏障限制汇聚在一起,使症状后的救援在生物学上变得困难。狂犬病病毒 首先通过进入周围神经、利用受体支持的摄取和逆行轴突运输以及在受保护的神经回路内建立感染来改变解剖战场。然后,它通过微弱或依赖菌株的先天感应、病毒干扰干扰素和信号转导器和转录激活剂 (STAT) 信号传导以及外周免疫激活不充分转化为有效的中枢神经系统清除来延迟 宿主 反击。一旦中枢神经系统(CNS)感染建立,血脑屏障限制进一步限制中和抗体、抗病毒药物化合物、生物制剂和免疫细胞进入受感染的神经组织。这些屏障的汇聚使得中枢神经系统持续存在,并导致致命性脑病,其特征不是广泛的神经元破坏,而是突触、树突、回路、自主神经和功能崩溃。最近的实验进展,包括中枢神经系统可及抗体策略、血脑屏障 (BBB) 调节方法、单克隆抗体治疗和单一药物模型,表明有症状的 狂犬病 可能需要阶段特异性的治疗组合,而不是单一的救援干预。我们建议未来的 狂犬病 治疗应围绕三个转化窗口进行组织:在 CNS 进入之前预防神经侵袭,在早期 CNS 感染期间实现免疫辅助病毒控制,以及一旦脑病确立,将 CNS 病毒清除与神经保护和自主神经支持相结合。然而,大多数机制和治疗证据仍然来自细胞和动物模型,针对有症状的人类 狂犬病 的中枢神经系统定向或针对屏障的干预措施的临床验证仍然有限。打破级联屏障可能是使有症状的 狂犬病 可治疗的核心要求。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
狂犬病病毒 和 狂犬病 疾病。该综述重点关注导致有症状的 狂犬病 极高死亡率的生物屏障,包括神经侵袭、免疫逃避和血脑屏障限制。核心问题是这些障碍如何使症状后的救援在生物学上变得困难。
证据基础与方法
该综述是对现有文献的叙述性综合,没有明确提及系统搜索方法、数据库或荟萃分析。证据来自实验研究,包括细胞和动物模型以及临床观察。
共识与结论
该综述的结论是,狂犬病病毒 会导致连续的屏障驱动的疾病过程,其中神经侵袭、免疫逃避和血脑屏障限制汇聚在一起,使症状后的救援在生物学上变得困难。一旦中枢神经系统(CNS)感染发生,血脑屏障就会限制治疗药物进入受感染的神经组织,从而导致致命性脑病。
争议与缺口
该评论指出,大多数机制和治疗证据仍然来自细胞和动物模型,对有症状的人类 狂犬病 的中枢神经系统定向或针对屏障的干预措施的临床验证有限。关于人类 狂犬病 免疫逃避和血脑屏障限制的确切机制也缺乏共识。
科研与实践启示
该综述建议,未来的 狂犬病 治疗应围绕三个转化窗口进行组织:在进入中枢神经系统之前预防神经侵袭,在中枢神经系统感染早期实现免疫辅助病毒控制,以及一旦脑病确立,将中枢神经系统病毒清除与神经保护和自主神经支持相结合。它还强调需要对针对中枢神经系统或障碍的干预措施进行临床验证。

Biological barriers underlying the extremely high fatality of symptomatic rabies: neuroinvasion, immune evasion, and blood–brain barrier restriction

Authors: Chen Sun, Xiao Guo, Liang Xiao, Wenxin Wang, Ge Shi, Chenci Liu, Shunyao Chen, Zhao-Hui Tang
Original abstract (English)
Rabies remains one of the most preventable fatal infections, yet once clinical symptoms appear, survival is exceedingly rare. This paradox cannot be explained simply by the absence of a single effective antiviral drug. In this review, we frame symptomatic rabies as a sequential barrier-driven disease process in which neuroinvasion, immune evasion, and blood–brain barrier restriction converge to make post-symptom rescue biologically difficult. Rabies virus first changes the anatomical battlefield by entering peripheral nerves, exploiting receptor-supported uptake and retrograde axonal transport, and establishing infection within protected neural circuits. It then delays the host counterattack through weak or strain-dependent innate sensing, viral interference with interferon and signal transducer and activator of transcription (STAT) signaling, and insufficient conversion of peripheral immune activation into effective central nervous system clearance. Once central nervous system (CNS) infection is established, blood–brain barrier restriction further limits access of neutralizing antibodies, antiviral compounds, biologics, and immune cells to infected neural tissue. The convergence of these barriers permits CNS persistence and drives fatal encephalopathy, characterized less by widespread neuronal destruction than by synaptic, dendritic, circuit, autonomic, and functional collapse. Recent experimental advances, including CNS-accessible antibody strategies, blood–brain barrier (BBB)-modulating approaches, monoclonal antibody therapy, and One Medicine models, suggest that symptomatic rabies may require stage-specific therapeutic combinations rather than a single rescue intervention. We propose that future rabies therapy should be organized around three translational windows: preventing neuroinvasion before CNS entry, achieving immune-assisted viral control during early CNS infection, and combining CNS viral clearance with neuroprotective and autonomic support once encephalopathy is established. However, most mechanistic and therapeutic evidence remains derived from cellular and animal models, and clinical validation of CNS-directed or barrier-targeted interventions in symptomatic human rabies remains limited. Breaking the barrier cascade may be the central requirement for making symptomatic rabies treatable.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Rabies virus and rabies disease. The review focuses on the biological barriers that contribute to the extremely high fatality of symptomatic rabies, including neuroinvasion, immune evasion, and blood–brain barrier restriction. The central question is how these barriers make post-symptom rescue biologically difficult.
Evidence base and method
The review is a narrative synthesis of existing literature, with no explicit mention of systematic search methods, databases, or meta-analysis. The evidence is derived from experimental studies, including cellular and animal models, and clinical observations.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that rabies virus causes a sequential barrier-driven disease process, where neuroinvasion, immune evasion, and blood–brain barrier restriction converge to make post-symptom rescue biologically difficult. Once central nervous system (CNS) infection is established, the blood–brain barrier restricts access of therapeutic agents to infected neural tissue, leading to fatal encephalopathy.
Controversies and gaps
The review notes that most mechanistic and therapeutic evidence remains derived from cellular and animal models, with limited clinical validation of CNS-directed or barrier-targeted interventions in symptomatic human rabies. There is also a lack of consensus on the exact mechanisms of immune evasion and blood–brain barrier restriction in human rabies.
Research and practice implications
The review suggests that future rabies therapy should be organized around three translational windows: preventing neuroinvasion before CNS entry, achieving immune-assisted viral control during early CNS infection, and combining CNS viral clearance with neuroprotective and autonomic support once encephalopathy is established. It also highlights the need for clinical validation of interventions targeting the CNS or barriers.

与巴塞罗那国际旅客 狂犬病 暴露后预防 延误相关的因素

Factors associated with delays in rabies post-exposure prophylaxis among international travelers in Barcelona
作者： Luis A Benegas, Valeria Pérez-Muto, Eva Calderón, Cristina Casals, Cristina M García-Blázquez, Marta Aldea, Guillermo Mena
摘要中文翻译狂犬病 是致命的 人兽共患病,通常可以通过提示 暴露后预防 (PEP) 来预防。前往流行地区的国际旅行者可能会遭受动物相关伤害 (AAI),但西班牙关于 PEP 启动延迟的数据仍然很少。我们对 2021 年 1 月 1 日至 2025 年 3 月 31 日期间在国外维持 AAI 后到巴塞罗那医院诊所成人 疫苗接种 中心就诊的国际旅行者进行了一项回顾性观察研究。我们审查了电子健康记录和 疫苗接种 登记处,以收集社会人口统计数据、旅行详细信息、AAI 特征、疫苗接种 历史记录(包括 PEP)和旅行前建议。结果为早期 疫苗接种(距 AAI < 1 天)和延迟 疫苗接种(≥ 3 天)。进行双变量和多变量逻辑回归分析,并使用 Cox 回归进行敏感性分析。共有 203 名旅客符合纳入标准。中位年龄为 31 岁(IQR 26-40),其中 49% 为女性,40% 为外国出生。大多数患者 (89%) 从未接受过 狂犬病 疫苗接种,但 19% 报告了旅行前建议。最常见的接触是咬伤(75%),主要是被狗咬伤(44%),主要发生在东南亚(32%)。首次 疫苗 剂量的中位延迟为 1 天(IQR 0-6);26% 接受了免疫球蛋白治疗。延迟的 疫苗接种 与之前的 疫苗接种 和 AAI 在不同于东南亚或东地中海的地区相关。之前未接种过疫苗且男性与早期接种疫苗的几率较高相关。敏感性分析支持了这些发现。虽然大多数旅行者在接触病毒后不久就开始了 疫苗接种,但延误很频繁,并且与个人特征有关。这些发现强调需要改进旅行前咨询、风险沟通和暴露后管理。需要进行更大规模的、具有标准化定义和创新分析的多中心研究,以产生更有力的证据并优化国际旅行者中 狂犬病 的预防策略。

范围与问题
狂犬病 是致命的 人兽共患病,通常可以通过提示 暴露后预防 (PEP) 来预防。最常见的接触是咬伤(75%),主要是被狗咬伤(44%),主要发生在东南亚(32%)。
证据基础与方法
我们对 2021 年 1 月 1 日至 2025 年 3 月 31 日期间在国外维持 AAI 后到巴塞罗那医院诊所成人 疫苗接种 中心就诊的国际旅行者进行了一项回顾性观察研究。进行双变量和多变量逻辑回归分析,并使用 Cox 回归进行敏感性分析。
共识与结论
大多数患者 (89%) 从未接受过 狂犬病 疫苗接种,但 19% 报告了旅行前建议。延迟的 疫苗接种 与之前的 疫苗接种 和 AAI 在不同于东南亚或东地中海的地区相关。之前未接种过疫苗且男性与早期接种疫苗的几率较高相关。敏感性分析支持了这些发现。
争议与缺口
首次 疫苗 剂量的中位延迟为 1 天(IQR 0-6);26% 接受了免疫球蛋白治疗。虽然大多数旅行者在接触病毒后不久就开始了 疫苗接种,但延误很频繁,并且与个人特征有关。这些发现强调需要改进旅行前咨询、风险沟通和暴露后管理。
科研与实践启示
需要进行更大规模的、具有标准化定义和创新分析的多中心研究,以产生更有力的证据并优化国际旅行者中 狂犬病 的预防策略。

Factors associated with delays in rabies post-exposure prophylaxis among international travelers in Barcelona

Authors: Luis A Benegas, Valeria Pérez-Muto, Eva Calderón, Cristina Casals, Cristina M García-Blázquez, Marta Aldea, Guillermo Mena
Original abstract (English)
Rabies is a fatal zoonosis that is usually preventable by prompt post-exposure prophylaxis (PEP). International travellers to endemic areas may sustain animal-associated injuries (AAIs), but data on delays in PEP initiation remain scarce in Spain. We conducted a retrospective observational study of international travellers who attended the Adult Vaccination Centre of Hospital Clínic Barcelona between 1 January 2021 and 31 March 2025 after sustaining an AAI abroad. Electronic health records and vaccination registries were reviewed to collect sociodemographic data, travel details, AAI characteristics, vaccination history including PEP, and pre-travel advice. Outcomes were early vaccination (< 1 day from AAI) and delayed vaccination (≥ 3 days). Bivariate and multivariate logistic regression analyses were conducted, with sensitivity analysis using Cox regression. A total of 203 travellers met inclusion criteria. Median age was 31 years (IQR 26–40), 49% were female and 40% foreign-born. Most patients (89%) had never received rabies vaccination, but 19% reported pre-travel advice. The most frequent exposures were bites (75%), mainly from dogs (44%), occurring predominantly in South-East Asia (32%). The median delay to first vaccine dose was 1 day (IQR 0–6); 26% received immunoglobulin. Delayed vaccination was associated with previous vaccination and AAIs in a region different from South-East Asia or Eastern Mediterranean. Not having been previously vaccinated and male sex were associated with higher odds of early initiation. Sensitivity analysis supported these findings. While most travellers began vaccination soon after exposure, delays were frequent and linked to individual characteristics. These findings highlight the need for improved pre-travel counselling, risk communication and post-exposure management. Larger multicentric studies with standardised definitions and innovative analyses are warranted to generate stronger evidence and optimise preventive strategies for rabies in international travellers.

Evidence base and method

Delayed vaccination was associated with previous vaccination and AAIs in a region different from South-East Asia or Eastern Mediterranean. Not having been previously vaccinated and male sex were associated with higher odds of early initiation. Sensitivity analysis supported these findings.
Controversies and gaps
The median delay to first vaccine dose was 1 day (IQR 0–6); 26% received immunoglobulin. While most travellers began vaccination soon after exposure, delays were frequent and linked to individual characteristics. These findings highlight the need for improved pre-travel counselling, risk communication and post-exposure management.
Research and practice implications
Larger multicentric studies with standardised definitions and innovative analyses are warranted to generate stronger evidence and optimise preventive strategies for rabies in international travellers.

暴露后预防指南遵循情况及与疫苗系列完成相关的因素分析:急诊科就诊且具有狂犬病风险暴露患者的调查

Guideline Adherence of Post-Exposure Prophylaxis and Factors Associated with Vaccine Series Completion in Patients Presenting to the Emergency Department with Rabies-Risk Exposure
作者： Ali Ekber Karabulut, Burcu Özen Karabulut
摘要中文翻译目的:本研究旨在回顾性评估对急诊科就诊的 狂犬病 暴露后预防 (PEP) 患者进行 狂犬病 暴露后预防 (PEP) 国家指南的遵守情况,并确定与完成 疫苗 系列相关的因素。方法:2025 年 6 月至 5 月期间到 Ağrı 培训和研究医院急诊科就诊的 343 名患者的记录2026年进行了审查。使用 Shapiro-Wilk 检验评估连续变量的分布;Mann-Whitney U、Kruskal-Wallis、卡方和 Fisher 精确检验用于组间比较。评估了所实施预防措施对 2019 年土耳其卫生部 狂犬病 预防指南的遵守情况。结果:患者的中位年龄为 21.0 岁(四分位距 [IQR]:10.0-34.0),62.1% 为男性。所采取预防措施的总体指南依从率为 54.5%;在需要使用 狂犬病 免疫球蛋白 (RIG) 的 III/IV 类暴露中,仅 14.0% 的病例使用了 RIG。疫苗系列的完成率为72.9%,在检查的14个变量中,仅发现既往预防史与完成率显着相关(p=0.014)。农村居住、非典型暴露类型和较轻的伤口类别与到急诊室就诊的较长时间延迟有关,并且在夏季,就诊时间明显增加。结论:狂犬病 暴露后预防 实践对国家指南的遵守情况,特别是关于 狂犬病 免疫球蛋白的适应症,被发现仍低于所需水平。加强医生教育并审查该地区 RIG 的可及性被认为是必要的

范围与问题
本研究旨在回顾性评估对急诊科就诊的 狂犬病 风险暴露患者进行 狂犬病 暴露后预防 (PEP) 的国家指南的遵守情况,并确定与完成 疫苗 系列相关的因素。
证据基础与方法
使用 Shapiro-Wilk 检验评估连续变量的分布;Mann-Whitney U、Kruskal-Wallis、卡方和 Fisher 精确检验用于组间比较。
共识与结论
评估了所实施预防措施对 2019 年土耳其卫生部 狂犬病 预防指南的遵守情况。所采取预防措施的总体指南依从率为 54.5%;在需要使用 狂犬病 免疫球蛋白 (RIG) 的 III/IV 类暴露中,仅 14.0% 的病例使用了 RIG。发现 狂犬病 暴露后预防 实践对国家指南的遵守情况,特别是关于 狂犬病 免疫球蛋白的适应症,仍低于所需水平。

Guideline Adherence of Post-Exposure Prophylaxis and Factors Associated with Vaccine Series Completion in Patients Presenting to the Emergency Department with Rabies-Risk Exposure

Authors: Ali Ekber Karabulut, Burcu Özen Karabulut
Original abstract (English)
Objective: This study aimed to retrospectively evaluate adherence to national guidelines in rabies post-exposure prophylaxis (PEP) administered to patients presenting with rabies-risk exposures to the emergency department, and to identify factors associated with completion of the vaccine series.Methods: Records of 343 patients presenting to the Emergency Department of Ağrı Training and Research Hospital between June 2025 and May 2026 were reviewed. The distribution of continuous variables was assessed using the Shapiro-Wilk test; the Mann-Whitney U, Kruskal-Wallis, chi-square, and Fisher's exact tests were used for between-group comparisons. Adherence of the administered prophylaxis to the 2019 Turkish Ministry of Health Rabies Prophylaxis Guideline was evaluated.Results: The median age of patients was 21.0 years (interquartile range [IQR]: 10.0-34.0), and 62.1% were male. Overall guideline adherence of the administered prophylaxis was found to be 54.5%; among Category III/IV exposures with an indication for rabies immunoglobulin (RIG), RIG was administered in only 14.0% of cases. The vaccine series completion rate was 72.9%, and among the 14 variables examined, only a history of prior prophylaxis was found to be significantly associated with completion (p=0.014). Rural residence, atypical exposure types, and milder wound categories were associated with longer delays in presentation to the emergency department, and presentations increased markedly during summer months.Conclusion: Adherence of rabies post-exposure prophylaxis practices to national guidelines, particularly regarding the indication for rabies immunoglobulin, was found to remain below the desired level. Strengthening physician education and reviewing RIG accessibility in the region were considered necessary
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This study aimed to retrospectively evaluate adherence to national guidelines in rabies post-exposure prophylaxis (PEP) administered to patients presenting with rabies-risk exposures to the emergency department, and to identify factors associated with completion of the vaccine series.
Evidence base and method
The distribution of continuous variables was assessed using the Shapiro-Wilk test; the Mann-Whitney U, Kruskal-Wallis, chi-square, and Fisher's exact tests were used for between-group comparisons.
Consensus and conclusions
Adherence of the administered prophylaxis to the 2019 Turkish Ministry of Health Rabies Prophylaxis Guideline was evaluated. Overall guideline adherence of the administered prophylaxis was found to be 54.5%; among Category III/IV exposures with an indication for rabies immunoglobulin (RIG), RIG was administered in only 14.0% of cases. Adherence of rabies post-exposure prophylaxis practices to national guidelines, particularly regarding the indication for rabies immunoglobulin, was found to remain below the desired level.
Controversies and gaps
The vaccine series completion rate was 72.9%, and among the 14 variables examined, only a history of prior prophylaxis was found to be significantly associated with completion (p=0.014). Rural residence, atypical exposure types, and milder wound categories were associated with longer delays in presentation to the emergency department, and presentations increased markedly during summer months.
Research and practice implications
Strengthening physician education and reviewing RIG accessibility in the region were considered necessary.

妊娠期 疫苗:指南回顾

Vaccines in Pregnancy: A Review of Guidelines
作者： Melissa E. Chambers
摘要中文翻译摘要重要性:疫苗接种 在怀孕期间可以保护孕妇和胎儿免受严重传染病的侵害。过去十年来,随着新的 疫苗、可靠的安全数据以及通过胎盘 IgG 转移对新生儿有益的明确证据,孕产妇免疫接种范围不断扩大。目的:总结有关妊娠前和妊娠期间服用 疫苗 的安全性、有效性和临床建议的当前证据,概述解决 疫苗 犹豫的策略,并提供妊娠期间推荐和禁忌的 疫苗 的最新参考。证据获取:证据综合自 CDC、ACOG、SMFM 发布的指南和主要同行评审出版物,包括随机试验、观察性研究、疫苗 安全性 监测 系统和荟萃分析。还回顾了与 疫苗 可预防疾病相关的疾病负担、免疫生理学和围产期结局的文献。结果:流感、Tdap 和 COVID-19 疫苗 显示出强大的孕产妇和新生儿保护作用,且安全性优异。RSV 疫苗 为婴儿提供额外的保护,预防严重的下呼吸道疾病。当有临床指征时,其他几种 疫苗(例如甲型和乙型肝炎、脑膜炎球菌、脊髓灰质炎、狂犬病)可以在妊娠期间施用。减毒活 疫苗 仍然是禁忌的。将孕产妇免疫接种纳入常规产前护理并提供现场服务时最为有效。疫苗 的犹豫可以通过循证咨询、解决安全问题和改善提供者与患者的信任关系来缓解。结论:母亲免疫是安全、有效的,对于预防孕妇和婴儿的发病和 死亡率 至关重要。相关性:本综述为临床医生在怀孕前、怀孕期间和怀孕后提供咨询、管理和优化 疫苗 使用的最新指南。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述重点关注 狂犬病病毒 和 狂犬病 疾病,检查 疫苗 在妊娠期间针对这些目标的安全性、有效性和临床建议。它还提出了减少 疫苗 犹豫的策略,并提供了在怀孕前、怀孕期间和怀孕后使用 疫苗 的最新指南。
证据基础与方法
证据综合自 CDC、ACOG、SMFM 发布的指南和主要同行评审出版物。其中包括随机试验、观察性研究、疫苗 安全性 监测 系统和荟萃分析。该综述整合了这些来源,以评估 疫苗 的安全性、有效性以及妊娠期 狂犬病病毒 和 狂犬病 疾病的临床建议。
共识与结论
流感、Tdap 和 COVID-19 疫苗 表现出强大的孕产妇和新生儿保护作用,并具有出色的安全性。RSV 疫苗 为婴儿提供额外的保护,预防严重的下呼吸道疾病。当有临床指征时,其他几种 疫苗(例如甲型和乙型肝炎、脑膜炎球菌、脊髓灰质炎、狂犬病)可以在妊娠期间施用。将孕产妇免疫接种纳入常规产前护理并提供现场服务时最为有效。疫苗 的犹豫可以通过循证咨询、解决安全问题和改善提供者与患者的信任关系来缓解。
争议与缺口
减毒活 疫苗 仍然是禁忌的。该综述没有报告与妊娠期 狂犬病病毒 和 狂犬病 疾病相关的具体争议或证据差距,因为证据集中在 疫苗 的安全性和有效性上。
科研与实践启示
母亲免疫接种是安全、有效的,对于预防孕妇和婴儿的发病和 死亡率 至关重要。本综述为临床医生在怀孕前、怀孕期间和怀孕后提供咨询、管理和优化 疫苗 使用的最新指南。

Vaccines in Pregnancy: A Review of Guidelines

Authors: Melissa E. Chambers
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Importance: Vaccination during pregnancy protects both the pregnant individual and the fetus from severe infectious diseases. Maternal immunization has expanded over the past decade, with new vaccines, robust safety data, and clear evidence of neonatal benefit through transplacental IgG transfer. Objective: To summarize current evidence regarding the safety, efficacy, and clinical recommendations for vaccines administered before and during pregnancy, outline strategies to address vaccine hesitancy, and provide an updated reference of vaccines recommended and contraindicated during pregnancy. Evidence Acquisition: Evidence synthesized from guidelines issued by the CDC, ACOG, SMFM, and major peer-reviewed publications including randomized trials, observational studies, vaccine safety surveillance systems, and meta-analyses. Literature informing disease burden, immune physiology, and perinatal outcomes associated with vaccine-preventable diseases also reviewed. Results: Influenza, Tdap, and COVID-19 vaccines demonstrate strong maternal and neonatal protective effects with excellent safety profiles. The RSV vaccine offers additional infant protection against severe lower respiratory tract disease. Several other vaccines (e.g., hepatitis A and B, meningococcal, polio, rabies) can be administered in pregnancy when clinically indicated. Live-attenuated vaccines remain contraindicated. Maternal immunization is most effective when integrated into routine prenatal care with onsite availability. Vaccine hesitancy can be mitigated through evidence-based counseling, addressing safety concerns, and improving the provider-patient trust relationship. Conclusion: Maternal immunization is safe, effective, and essential for preventing morbidity and mortality among pregnant individuals and infants. Relevance: This review provides clinicians with an updated guide for counseling, administering, and optimizing vaccine use before, during, and after pregnancy.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on rabies virus and rabies disease, examining the safety, efficacy, and clinical recommendations for vaccines against these targets during pregnancy. It also addresses strategies to reduce vaccine hesitancy and provides updated guidance on vaccine use before, during, and after pregnancy.
Evidence base and method
The evidence is synthesized from guidelines issued by the CDC, ACOG, SMFM, and major peer-reviewed publications. These include randomized trials, observational studies, vaccine safety surveillance systems, and meta-analyses. The review integrates these sources to evaluate vaccine safety, efficacy, and clinical recommendations for rabies virus and rabies disease in pregnancy.
Consensus and conclusions
Influenza, Tdap, and COVID-19 vaccines demonstrate strong maternal and neonatal protective effects with excellent safety profiles. The RSV vaccine offers additional infant protection against severe lower respiratory tract disease. Several other vaccines (e.g., hepatitis A and B, meningococcal, polio, rabies) can be administered in pregnancy when clinically indicated. Maternal immunization is most effective when integrated into routine prenatal care with onsite availability. Vaccine hesitancy can be mitigated through evidence-based counseling, addressing safety concerns, and improving the provider-patient trust relationship.
Controversies and gaps
Live-attenuated vaccines remain contraindicated. The review does not report on specific controversies or evidence gaps related to rabies virus and rabies disease in pregnancy, as the evidence is focused on the safety and efficacy of vaccines.
Research and practice implications
Maternal immunization is safe, effective, and essential for preventing morbidity and mortality among pregnant individuals and infants. This review provides clinicians with an updated guide for counseling, administering, and optimizing vaccine use before, during, and after pregnancy.

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

研究发现,美国超过 90% 的人类 狂犬病 病例未被发现 - ScienceAlert

Over 90% of Human Rabies Cases in The US Aren't Detected, Study Finds - ScienceAlert
新闻简报《Communications Health》发表的一项新研究表明,美国严重低估了人类 狂犬病 病例。牛津大学流行病科学研究所的研究人员表示,官方记录显示,从2000年到2024年,每年只有2.4例确诊病例,但统计分析表明,同期应该有大约856例病例。这种差异意味着超过 90% 的人类 狂犬病 病例可能未被发现。该研究强调了通过器官移植传播 狂犬病 传播 的风险,并引用密歇根州的病例作为已知的第四例受感染的捐赠者将病毒传播给接受者的案例。CDC 指出,由于存活所需的时间窗口很短,因此 狂犬病 测试并不常规用于器官移植。研究人员警告说,他们的估计是基于统计假设,没有考虑地理或职业等因素,这些因素可能会完善他们的预测。该调查结果引发了人们对美国 狂犬病 监测 完整性的担忧。
查看新闻五要素
时间
该研究于2026-09-01发表。事件日期未明确说明,但引用了密歇根患者病例作为近期事件。分析涵盖2000–2024年。
地点与对象
美国,重点是密歇根州和爱达荷州。受影响的人群包括器官接受者和已故捐赠者,特别是那些死于 狂犬病 相关原因的人。
事件
《Communications Health》发表的一项研究表明,官方记录大大低估了美国人类 狂犬病 病例的数量。从 2000 年到 2024 年,CDC 平均每年仅诊断出 2.4 例病例,但研究人员估计,超过 90% 的病例未被发现,预计确诊病例约为 856 例,而确诊病例为 60 例。
规模、影响与风险
该研究估计,从 2000 年到 2024 年,美国发生了大约 856 例人类 狂犬病 病例,但只有 60 例得到证实。这意味着超过 90% 的病例可能会被遗漏。通过器官移植传播 狂犬病 传播 的风险得到了强调,密歇根州的病例是已知的第四例此类 传播 病例。
应对、状态与不确定性
CDC 承认,由于时间限制,狂犬病 检测并未常规用于器官移植。该研究的结果基于统计分析,并未考虑地理、职业或社会经济因素。研究人员强调,他们的估计并不是确定的,有待进一步调查。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Over 90% of Human Rabies Cases in The US Aren't Detected, Study Finds - ScienceAlert

News Brief
A new study published in Communications Health suggests that the United States significantly underreports human rabies cases. According to researchers from the Pandemic Sciences Institute at the University of Oxford, official records show only 2.4 confirmed cases annually from 2000 to 2024, but statistical analysis indicates that there should have been around 856 cases during the same period. This discrepancy implies that over 90% of human rabies cases may go undetected. The study highlights the risk of rabies transmission through organ transplants, citing the Michigan case as the fourth known instance where an infected donor transmitted the virus to a recipient. The CDC notes that rabies testing is not routinely conducted for organ transplants due to the short time window required for viability. The researchers caution that their estimates are based on statistical assumptions and do not consider factors like geography or occupation, which could refine their projections. The findings raise concerns about the completeness of rabies surveillance in the US.
Original source text (English)
click Space Health Environment Humans Tech Nature Physics TWIS About Us Our Team Follow Us Big breakthroughs. Bold ideas. Straight to your inbox. Daily roundup Spark: Our top stories Over 90% of Human Rabies Cases in The US Aren't Detected, Study Finds Health01 September 2026 By Peter Dockrill Add ScienceAlert on Google Colorized transmission electron micrograph of rabies virus particles. (NIAID/Wikimedia Commons/CC BY 2.0) In February of last year, authorities confirmed the details of a grave medical accident. The US Centers for Disease Control and Prevention (CDC) diagnosed a fatal rabies infection in a patient from Michigan. The circumstances of transmission were unusual. The deceased man, an organ recipient, had received a donated kidney infected with rabies. The source of the kidney was a man in Idaho who had died only weeks earlier, donating his organs for others in need. At the time of his death, nobody realized his body was infected with the deadly virus. Subsequent investigations revealed the Idaho man had recently been scratched by a skunk on his rural property – an infectious encounter that ultimately claimed his own life, and that of the man to whom he gave his kidney. Records show it was only the fourth time in US history that an organ transplant procedure had transmitted the rabies virus (Lyssavirus rabies) from an initially undiagnosed donor. But what if these kinds of records are wrong? Colorized transmission electron micrograph of rabies virus particles. (NIAID/Wikimedia Commons/CC BY 2.0) A new analysis published in Communications Health suggests that official figures may dramatically underestimate how many human cases of rabies are actually circulating in the US each year. According to researchers from the Pandemic Sciences Institute at the University of Oxford, the extremely low count of confirmed human rabies cases in the US each year – just 2.4 cases annually on average from 2000 to 2024 – seems much lower than it should be. The reason why it seems low is that a relatively high number of rabies cases are subsequently confirmed after infected organ transplants, like the sad case of the kidney recipient from Michigan. frameborder="0′′ allow="accelerometer; autoplay; clipboard-write; encrypted-media; gyroscope; picture-in-picture; web-share" referrerpolicy="strict-origin-when-cross-origin" allowfullscreen> Even though the Michigan patient fatality represents only the fourth known case of a transplanted fatal rabies infection – the first such instance occurring in 1978 – given how rare organ donation itself is, something just doesn't add up. "The instances of rabies transmission from initially undiagnosed deceased donors suggest that many human deaths due to rabies are not diagnosed as such, considering that only a small percentage of people dying in the US become organ and tissue donors," the researchers explain in a comment article. Per the researchers' calculations – which assume that the infected donors we know about faced an average nationwide level of risk of being infected with rabies – there should have been about 856 human cases of rabies in the years 2000–2024, whereas official records only document 60 confirmed cases during the period. In other words, from a purely statistical point of view, confirmed cases of human rabies that are recorded by the CDC may have missed over 90 percent of cases since the turn of the millennium, amounting to about 30 cases being missed each year on average. "Our central estimate suggests that only around 7 percent of human rabies cases in the general population were confirmed in recent years, and even its confidence limits indicate that, in the best-case scenario, only around 22 percent were diagnosed," the researchers write. "In order to argue that the reported number of rabies cases is consistent with complete surveillance, deceased donors would need to have a rabies risk approximately 14 times that of decedents in the general population." It's worth emphasizing that the researchers' calculations are only estimates based on statistical data, and they acknowledge they didn't take into account geographical, occupational, or socioeconomic factors in their analysis of rabies risk, which could help refine their projections. While it might seem unlikely that dozens of cases of rabies are being missed by doctors each year, that's exactly the scenario that plays out in the case of the infected organ donors, with the causes of their deaths only being discovered tragically late in each instance. In the case of the Idaho man, before his death he had difficulty swallowing and walking, experienced hallucinations, and was later found unresponsive at home after a presumed cardiac arrest. Related: US Man Dies From Rabies After Receiving Infected Kidney In another rabies-infected case reported in 2005, the donor experienced nausea, vomiting, brain hemorrhaging, and seizures before death. Later, when his rabies infection was discovered, his friends recalled he had mentioned being bitten by a bat. In both cases, though, nobody at the time recognized the fatal infection for what it was, presumably because the symptoms could have been explained by many other possible (and likely more probable) causes. For similar reasons, routine lab tests for organ transplants don't screen for rabies, both because it's a niche case and also because of the time pressure during transplant situations. "If rabies is not clinically suspected, laboratory testing for rabies is not routinely performed," the CDC explained in 2013, "as it is difficult for doctors to confirm results in the short window of time they have to keep the organs viable for the recipient." The findings are reported in Communications Health. This article was fact-checked by Rebecca Dyer and edited by Rebecca Dyer. While we pride ourselves on our process, we are only human. B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

农业部记录 15 人死亡 狂犬病

Department of Agriculture records 15 human rabies deaths
新闻简报南非农业部报告自今年年初以来已有 15 人因 狂犬病 死亡,其中东开普省、昆士兰州和林波波省有重大病例。这些死亡包括东开普省的 7 例确诊病例和 2 例疑似病例、夸赞省的 5 例确诊病例和林波波省的 1 例确诊病例。暴发 强调了 狂犬病 在所有九个省份的严重威胁,2026 年 1 月 1 日至 7 月 31 日期间记录了 115 例实验室阳性动物病例。当局敦促宠物主人为动物接种疫苗并负责任地饲养宠物,以防止进一步传播。他们还与国家 狂犬病 咨询小组合作,以提高认识和预防措施。紧急方案包括立即清洗伤口以及在接触后就医 治疗。该应对措施强调社区、动物和人类健康部门以及政府之间的合作,以消除 狂犬病,正如 9 月 28 日世界 狂犬病 日的“更强”主题所强调的那样。
查看新闻五要素
时间
自年初起,至2026年7月31日。文章发表于2026年9月4日。
地点与对象
南非,横跨所有九个省份,其中林波波省、夸大和东开普省是重要的热点地区。受影响的人群包括人类和动物。
事件
据农业部称,南非记录了 15 人死亡 狂犬病。其中包括东开普省的 7 例确诊病例和 2 例疑似病例、夸赞省的 5 例确诊病例和林波波省的 1 例确诊病例。该事件是根据农业部的报告得到证实的。
规模、影响与风险
2026 年 1 月 1 日至 7 月 31 日期间,报告了 15 例人类 狂犬病 死亡病例,其中 115 例实验室检测呈阳性的动物 狂犬病 病例。该疾病仍然对所有省份构成严重威胁,其中林波波省、昆士兰州和东开普省是重要热点地区。该风险被明确表述为严重,死亡可以通过 疫苗接种 和暴露后 治疗 来预防。
应对、状态与不确定性
农业部敦促宠物主人为动物接种疫苗,并促进负责任的宠物饲养。他们正在与国家 狂犬病 咨询小组合作,提高认识并加强预防措施。紧急响应包括立即清洗受影响区域并在接触后寻求医疗救助 治疗。响应正在进行中,涉及多个利益相关者。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Department of Agriculture records 15 human rabies deaths

News Brief
The Department of Agriculture in South Africa reported 15 human rabies deaths since the start of the year, with significant cases in the Eastern Cape, KZN, and Limpopo. These deaths include seven confirmed and two probable cases in the Eastern Cape, five confirmed cases in KZN, and one confirmed case in Limpopo. The outbreak highlights the serious threat of rabies across all nine provinces, with 115 laboratory-positive animal cases recorded between January 1 and July 31, 2026. Authorities are urging pet owners to vaccinate animals and practice responsible pet ownership to prevent further spread. They are also working with the National Rabies Advisory Group to enhance awareness and prevention measures. Emergency protocols include immediate washing of wounds and seeking medical attention for post-exposure treatment. The response emphasizes collaboration between communities, animal and human health sectors, and governments to eliminate rabies, as highlighted by the 'Stronger Together' theme for World Rabies Day on September 28.
Original source text (English)
Product of Caxton Local Media Saturday, September 5 2026 Caxton Network News Menu Search for Home Network News Lifestyle Lifestyle News Get It Homes Motoring Motoring News Sport Business About Us Complaints Elections 2026 The Citizen Search for HomeLNN News ContentDepartment of Agriculture records 15 human rabies deaths Department of Agriculture records 15 human rabies deaths Authorities urge pet owners to vaccinate animals as World Rabies Day approaches. 15 hours ago SAnews.gov.za 2 minutes read Facebook X LinkedIn Tumblr Pinterest WhatsApp Telegram Share via Email Print An image of a puppy receiving a rabies vaccine for illustration purposes only. Photo: 123RF/Caxton Local Media South Africa has recorded 15 human rabies deaths since the start of the year, according to the Department of Agriculture. An article by George Herald notes that the reported human fatalities include seven confirmed and two probable cases in the Eastern Cape, five confirmed cases in KZN and one confirmed case in Limpopo. Between January 1 and July 31, officials recorded 115 laboratory-positive animal rabies cases. Hotspot provinces The disease remains a serious threat across all nine provinces, with significant hotspots in Limpopo, KZN and the Eastern Cape. Agriculture Minister Willie Aucamp said preventing rabies requires action from pet owners, communities, healthcare workers and authorities. “Vaccinating dogs and cats, practising responsible pet ownership, reporting suspected cases and seeking prompt medical care after potential exposure are essential to preventing deaths,” Aucamp said. Preventable illness Although rabies is almost always fatal once symptoms develop, deaths are preventable through appropriate vaccination and timely post-exposure treatment. The infection can affect any mammal, including domestic animals, livestock, wildlife and humans. Regular vaccination of dogs and cats remains one of the most effective ways to prevent the spread of rabies and protect households and communities. Responsible pet ownership includes preventing the movement of unvaccinated animals, monitoring animals for unusual behaviour and reporting suspected cases to authorities. Emergency response Anyone bitten or scratched by a suspected rabid animal, or exposed to its saliva through a wound or mucous membrane, must immediately wash the affected area with soap and running water for at least 10 minutes. Medical attention should be sought without delay, as post-exposure treatment can prevent rabies when administered promptly and correctly. Community action The department is working through the National Rabies Advisory Group with stakeholders to raise awareness and strengthen measures to protect animal and human health. Veterinary and para-veterinary professionals, healthcare workers, government departments, policymakers, communities, pet owners and animal welfare organisations all play a role in prevention. Members of the public can contact local state or private veterinarians, animal health technicians or animal welfare organisations for advice on prevention and vaccination. Details of provincial veterinary services are available through the Department of Agriculture. World Rabies Day South Africa will join countries worldwide to mark World Rabies Day on September 28. This year’s theme, ‘Stronger Together’, highlights the need for co-operation between communities, animal and human health sectors, governments and international partners to eliminate rabies. Aucamp urged South Africans to take practical steps to protect themselves, their families, pets and communities. “Through responsible pet ownership and acting quickly, we are ‘Stronger Together’ in the fight against rabies,” Aucamp said. WHO validates Bhutan for eliminating dog-transmitted human rabies - WebWire

News Brief
The World Health Organization (WHO) has validated Bhutan as having eliminated dog-transmitted human rabies, marking a significant public health achievement. This confirmation is based on comprehensive efforts to control rabies through vaccination programs and surveillance. The validation was announced on September 4, 2026, and highlights Bhutan's success in preventing rabies transmission from dogs to humans. No specific case counts, death counts, or exposure numbers were reported in the evidence provided. The WHO's validation indicates that the country has achieved a state where dog-transmitted rabies is no longer present, which is a critical milestone in global rabies elimination efforts. This achievement underscores the effectiveness of targeted interventions and collaboration with international health organizations.

(VIDEO) North Carolina Boy Faces Six Rabies Shots After Mobile Petting Zoo Goats Test Positive for the Virus - International Business Times Australia
新闻简报一名4岁的北卡罗来纳州男孩在接触宠物动物园的山羊后感染了狂犬病病毒,因此接受了六剂狂犬病治疗。这些山羊属于一家名为Farm Adventures的公司,其中三只被确认感染,另一只被怀疑感染。在发现感染后,这些动物被安乐死。男孩的姨妈于2026年8月25日告知其母亲Rebecca Lanning有关接触的情况,促使立即采取医疗措施。北卡罗来纳州卫生与人类服务部正与当地当局合作,确保任何可能接触过该病毒的人都能获得狂犬病(暴露后预防)药物。该公司表示,这些山羊很可能从浣熊身上感染了狂犬病病毒,而卫生部门尚未估计可能受影响的人数。一旦出现症状,狂犬病病毒几乎总是致命的,但如果在症状出现前使用疫苗,其有效性接近100%。此次事件突显了在接触野生动物或不熟悉的动物后及时寻求医疗帮助的重要性。
查看新闻五要素
时间
事件发生在2026年8月15日,当时男孩在宠物动物园与山羊互动。家人于 2026 年 8 月 25 日获悉暴露情况,男孩于 2026 年 8 月 25 日接受了前两次 狂犬病 注射。北卡罗来纳州卫生与公众服务部于 2026 年 8 月 25 日宣布了 狂犬病 病例。
地点与对象
该活动在北卡罗来纳州莫克斯维尔举行,涉及一个名为“农场冒险”的移动宠物动物园。受影响的人群包括这名男孩以及其他可能在2026年7月28日至8月15日期间出现在活动中与受感染山羊有过接触的人。
事件
北卡罗来纳州一名 4 岁男孩在移动宠物动物园抚摸小山羊后接触到了 狂犬病病毒。北卡罗来纳州卫生与公众服务部证实,三只山羊的 狂犬病 检测呈阳性,第四只山羊疑似感染。作为公共卫生应对措施的一部分,男孩收到了 暴露后预防 (PEP) 疫苗接种。
规模、影响与风险
这一事件促使北卡罗来纳州采取了更广泛的公共卫生应对措施,官员们正在努力识别并联系其他可能接触过病毒的人。卫生部门尚未估计可能暴露的人数。一旦出现症状,狂犬病 几乎总是致命的,但如果在症状出现之前施用,疫苗 几乎 100% 有效。
应对、状态与不确定性
北卡罗来纳州卫生与公众服务部正在与当地卫生部门和医疗保健系统合作,确保提供 狂犬病 PEP。Farm Adventures 公司在其 Facebook 页面上讨论了这一情况,称山羊可能是从臭鼬那里感染了 狂犬病。卫生部门尚未提供对暴露人数的估计,男孩与山羊的具体互动仍不确定。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

(VIDEO) North Carolina Boy Faces Six Rabies Shots After Mobile Petting Zoo Goats Test Positive for the Virus - International Business Times Australia

News Brief
A 4-year-old boy in North Carolina is receiving six rabies shots after being exposed to the virus through contact with baby goats at a mobile petting zoo. The goats, part of a company called Farm Adventures, tested positive for rabies, with three confirmed and a fourth suspected. The animals were euthanized following the discovery. The boy's aunt informed Rebecca Lanning, the mother, of the exposure on August 25, 2026, prompting immediate medical action. The North Carolina Department of Health and Human Services is working with local authorities to ensure post-exposure prophylaxis (PEP) is available to anyone who may have been exposed. The company stated the goats likely contracted rabies from a skunk, and the health department has not yet estimated the number of people potentially affected. Rabies is almost always fatal once symptoms appear, but the vaccine is nearly 100% effective if given before symptoms occur. The incident highlights the importance of prompt medical attention after contact with wild or unfamiliar animals.
Original source text (English)
INTERNATIONAL BUSINESS TIMES au NEWSLETTER SIGNUP My account Log Out Sep 06, 6:54 AM AEST Business News Sports Technology Entertainment NEWSLETTER My account Log Out Australia edition Business News Sports Technology Entertainment Editions Australia India International Singapore United Kingdom United States NEWSLETTER Follow Us Editions Australia India International Singapore United Kingdom United States Life Health & Wellbeing (VIDEO) North Carolina Boy Faces Six Rabies Shots After Mobile Petting Zoo Goats Test Positive for the Virus A child's encounter with rabid goats at a petting zoo prompts urgent health measures in North Carolina. By Anthony Will Published 09/02/26 AT 12:46 AM AEST Share on Facebook Share on Twitter Share on LinkedIn Share on Reddit Share on Flipboard Share on Pocket North Carolina Boy Faces Six Rabies Shots After Mobile Petting Zoo Goats Test Positive for the Virus MOCKSVILLE, N.C. — A North Carolina family is racing through a series of rabies vaccinations for their 4-year-old son after learning that baby goats he petted at a mobile petting zoo tested positive for the virus, part of a broader public health response now underway across the state. Rebecca Lanning, 30, of Marion, said she received a call from her son's aunt last Friday informing her that her son had been exposed to rabies. The family had visited a dinosaur-themed exhibit in Mocksville 13 days earlier, on Aug. 15, an event that also featured food trucks, carnival games and a petting zoo where the boy fed baby goats by hand. "I didn't think anything of it until Friday, when his aunt called me and was like, 'Hey, we have a situation,'" Lanning said. The North Carolina Department of Health and Human Services announced Friday that three baby goats from the petting zoo had tested positive for rabies. Those animals, along with a fourth goat suspected of carrying the virus, have since been euthanized. Lanning immediately took her son to the doctor, where he received the first two of six required doses of the rabies vaccine, a regimen known as post-exposure prophylaxis. The shots are administered after a possible exposure in an effort to stop the virus before it can take hold in the body. Once symptoms of a rabies infection begin, the disease, which attacks the brain and spinal cord, is almost always fatal. The vaccine is nearly 100% effective at preventing the disease, but only when it is administered before symptoms appear. People can become infected with rabies through contact with the saliva of an infected animal, or through a bite or scratch. Lanning said the uncertainty of her son's specific interactions with the goats has weighed on her heavily in the days since learning of the exposure. "My fear is that if he was feeding the goats, and he's 4, who's to say that he didn't touch his mouth or eyes?" Lanning said. "I don't want to bury my child." Rabies symptoms typically take weeks to months to appear after exposure, and can initially resemble the flu, including weakness, fatigue, fever and headaches. According to the North Carolina health department, people may also experience a prickling or itching sensation at the site where they were touched or bitten by an infected animal. As of Monday, the state health department said it did not have an estimate of how many people may have been exposed to the infected goats. The animals were part of a North Carolina-based mobile petting zoo operation called Farm Adventures, which had appeared at events across the state between July 28 and Aug. 23. Beyond the Mocksville dinosaur exhibit, the goats were also brought to private gatherings, including birthday parties and a back-to-school celebration, as well as a visit to an assisted living center. A representative for Farm Adventures did not respond to a request for comment. The company addressed the situation in a post on its Facebook page, stating that its goats, which were too young to have been vaccinated against rabies themselves, appeared to have contracted the virus after a skunk got into their enclosure. Skunks are among the animals most commonly known to carry and transmit rabies, along with bats, raccoons and foxes. In a statement, the North Carolina health department said it is "working with local health departments and healthcare systems to make sure rabies post-exposure prophylaxis is available to anyone who needs it," as officials work to identify and reach others who may have come into contact with the infected animals during their weeks on the road. The incident comes amid what public health officials say is a broader increase in rabies exposures nationally in recent years. According to the Centers for Disease Control and Prevention, roughly 1.4 million Americans are evaluated annually for possible rabies exposure, with about 100,000 people ultimately receiving post-exposure prophylaxis shots each year. Health officials continue to urge caution around wild and unfamiliar animals, along with prompt medical attention following any bite, scratch or direct contact with an animal's saliva, given how critical early treatment is to preventing the disease from becoming fatal. The incident occurred on August 15, 2026, when the boy interacted with the goats at the petting zoo. The family was informed of the exposure on August 25, 2026, and the boy received his first two rabies shots on August 25, 2026. The North Carolina Department of Health and Human Services announced the rabies cases on August 25, 2026.
Location and population
The event took place in Mocksville, North Carolina, involving a mobile petting zoo called Farm Adventures. The affected population includes the boy and potentially others who had contact with the infected goats during their appearances at events between July 28 and August 15, 2026.
Event
A 4-year-old boy in North Carolina was exposed to the rabies virus after petting baby goats at a mobile petting zoo. The North Carolina Department of Health and Human Services confirmed that three goats tested positive for rabies, and a fourth was suspected. The boy received post-exposure prophylaxis (PEP) vaccinations as part of the public health response.
Scale, impact and risk
The incident has prompted a broader public health response in North Carolina, with officials working to identify and reach others who may have been exposed. The health department has not yet estimated the number of people potentially exposed. Rabies is almost always fatal once symptoms appear, but the vaccine is nearly 100% effective if administered before symptoms occur.
Response, status and uncertainty
The North Carolina Department of Health and Human Services is collaborating with local health departments and healthcare systems to ensure rabies PEP is available. The company Farm Adventures addressed the situation on its Facebook page, stating the goats likely contracted rabies from a skunk. The health department has not yet provided an estimate of the number of people exposed, and the boy's specific interactions with the goats remain uncertain.

利文斯顿庄园的蝙蝠 狂犬病 检测呈阳性

Bat tests positive for rabies in Livingston Manor
新闻简报2026 年 9 月 5 日,沙利文县公共卫生部门证实,纽约州沙利文县利文斯顿庄园的一只蝙蝠检测出 狂犬病 呈阳性。这标志着该地区今年首例涉及实验室验证呈阳性动物的人类接触事件。如果不及时用 暴露后预防 治疗,狂犬病 是一种致命的疾病,当局建议避免接触野生动物。卫生官员已安排 9 月 16 日在 Narrowsburg Firehouse 开设 狂犬病 疫苗接种 诊所,需要预先登记。计划在罗克兰镇开设另一家诊所。敦促居民拨打 845-292-5910 向县卫生部门报告任何野生动物咬伤事件。这种疾病在纽约州的浣熊、蝙蝠、狐狸、臭鼬和土拨鼠中很常见。预防措施包括让宠物随时了解 狂犬病 疫苗接种 的最新情况,并注意攻击性行为、行走困难或流口水等迹象。
查看新闻五要素
时间
沙利文县公共卫生部门于 2026 年 9 月 5 日凌晨 4:48 确认了 狂犬病 阳性蝙蝠。疫苗接种 诊所定于 2026 年 9 月 16 日举行。
地点与对象
事件发生在纽约州沙利文县利文斯顿庄园。受影响的人群包括该地区的居民和宠物。
事件
沙利文县公共卫生部门证实,利文斯顿庄园的一只蝙蝠检测出 狂犬病病毒 呈阳性。这是今年首例涉及经实验室验证呈阳性动物的人类接触事件。
规模、影响与风险
Rabies is common throughout New York State, with the majority of cases in raccoons, bats, foxes, skunks, and woodchucks. Rabies is 100 percent fatal if not promptly treated with rabies post exposure prophylaxis. Authorities emphasize avoiding contact as the best way to protect against the disease.
应对、状态与不确定性
卫生官员建议向县卫生部门报告所有野生动物咬伤事件。疫苗接种 诊所已定于 9 月 16 日在 Narrowsburg Firehouse 举行,需要预先登记。罗克兰镇正在规划另一家诊所。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Bat tests positive for rabies in Livingston Manor

News Brief
A bat in Livingston Manor, Sullivan County, New York, tested positive for rabies, as confirmed by the Sullivan County Public Health Department on September 5, 2026. This marks the first confirmed human exposure incident involving a laboratory-verified positive animal in the region this year. Rabies is a fatal disease if not treated promptly with post-exposure prophylaxis, and authorities recommend avoiding contact with wild animals. Health officials have scheduled a rabies vaccination clinic for September 16 at the Narrowsburg Firehouse, with pre-registration required. Another clinic is planned for the Town of Rockland. Residents are urged to report any wild animal bites to the county health department at 845-292-5910. The disease is common in raccoons, bats, foxes, skunks, and woodchucks in New York State. Preventive measures include keeping pets up to date on rabies vaccinations and being aware of signs such as aggressive behavior, difficulty walking, or drooling.

This is the first confirmed human exposure incident involving a laboratory-verified positive animal this year. Rabies is common throughout New York State, with the vast majority of cases in raccoons, bats, foxes, skunks, and woodchucks. Authorities say avoiding contact is your absolute best way of protecting yourself from this fatal disease. Health officials say all wild animal bites should be reported to the county health department at 845-292-5910. Rabies is 100 percent fatal if not promptly treated with rabies post exposure prophylaxis. Officials also said pets should be kept up to date on rabies vaccinations. Another clinic is being scheduled in the Town of Rockland. Signs of rabies in wild animals include aggressive, uncharacteristic behavior, such as attacking objects of people; losing fear of humans; difficulty walking or staggering; paralysis of the legs or jaw, loss of coordination; difficulty swallowing and a fear of water; and drooling or foaming at the mouth.
